000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;  for error messages
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               .macro ERR_entry errmsg
000180r 2                   .byte <errmsg
000180r 2                   .byte >errmsg
000180r 2                   emcount .set emcount + 1
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               
000180r 2               .macro WERR errptr
000180r 2               ;.macro WERR
000180r 2                   .local werrloop, werrend
000180r 2                   WCRLF_np
000180r 2                   ldy #0
000180r 2                   lda (errptr),y
000180r 2                   sta TEMP0
000180r 2                   iny
000180r 2                   lda (errptr),y
000180r 2                   sta TEMP0+1
000180r 2                   ldy #0
000180r 2               werrloop:
000180r 2                   lda (TEMP0),y
000180r 2                   beq werrend
000180r 2                   iny
000180r 2                   jsr WRITE_CHAR
000180r 2                   bra werrloop
000180r 2               werrend:
000180r 2                   WCRLF_np
000180r 2                   rts
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;              library list  - handle like an error/token jumptable
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;                --- this is also how MS-BASIC knows where to send
000180r 2               ;   the code when a tokenized command is invoked.  Inherent limitation
000180r 2               ;   with one-byte tokens (for errors or libraries) is max of 255 entries.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               .macro LIBENTRY libname, bank
000180r 2                   .byte <libname
000180r 2                   .byte >libname
000180r 2                   .byte bank
000180r 2                   .byte 0
000180r 2                   libcount .set libcount + 1
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ; variables for macros
000180r 2               
000180r 2               hcount .set 0
000180r 2               emcount .set 0             ; # of error messages set up
000180r 2               libcount .set 0
000180r 2               
000180r 2               H0000 = 0
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;               CONFIGURATION OPTIONS
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; number conversion
000180r 2               numbers := 1      ; include DEC/BIN/HEX conversion
000180r 2               
000180r 2               SINGLE := 1     ; single digits hard coded?
000180r 2               
000180r 2               DEBUG := 1        ; enable inclusion of debug code
000180r 2               
000180r 2               HYWORDS := 1      ; add in additional hardcoded words / logic
000180r 2               
000180r 2               HFA := 1          ; HyForth assembler
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ANSIOK := 1         ; add ANSI screen stuff
000180r 2               
000180r 2               YSOUND := 1        ; add sound support
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;              for PGS hyforth stuff
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ; error codes
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ERR_RPTR := $01    ; return stack pointer error
000180r 2               ERR_SPTR := $02    ; data stack pointer error
000180r 2               ERR_DIV0 := $03    ; divide by zero
000180r 2               ERR_MEM := $04     ; memory not-avail
000180r 2               ERR_UKW := $05     ; unknown word
000180r 2               ERR_SEC := $06     ; security error, ie dangerous address write
000180r 2               ERR_SYS := $07     ; return from system call error
000180r 2               ERR_PAD := $08
000180r 2                 ; warnings
000180r 2               WRN_SEC := $86     ; security
000180r 2               WRN_MEM := $84     ; memory alloc
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;                 CORE ENGINE CONSTANTS
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               CELL = 2         ; cell size, two bytes, 16-bit
000180r 2               FLAG_IMM = 1<<7  ; immediate flag
000180r 2               FLAG_COM = 1<<6  ; compiled flag
000180r 2               MAXSTR = 100
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; terminal input buffer, forward
000180r 2               ; getline, token, skip, scan, depends on page boundary
000180r 2               ; INBUF = $0400  (see segment STACKS below)
000180r 2               ; moves forwards
000180r 2               INBUF_end = $FD
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; data stacks
000180r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
000180r 2               DSEND = $7E
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; return stack
000180r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
000180r 2               RTEND = $FE
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; malloc stack and managed memory area
000180r 2               ; both move downwards like other stacks
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               MEMEND = $01FE            ; pointers DOWN from MEMEND+MEMSTK (store-and-decrement)
000180r 2               MEMTOP = $7BF0            ; malloc allocates DOWN from MEMTOP
000180r 2               SCRBUF = $7C00            ; screen buffer 1K going up
000180r 2               MSBUF = $600             ; note, allocated at assembly, may change
000180r 2               PADBUF = $800            ; note, allocated at assembly, may change
000180r 2               
000180r 2               PADEND = $01FE            ; PAD+PADEND, up with PADPTR - increment and store
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;   types:  <type> <len0 len1> <data data data...>
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               MEM_SCR = $01  ; screen  (1024 bytes, with 63 char rows terminated either $09 or $00
000180r 2               MEM_SZ = $04   ; zero-termed string
000180r 2               MEM_FRAC = $06 ; 'fractional binary' <type> <03 00> <offset> <xxxx>
000180r 2               MEM_TAB = $07  ; table
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ; spi
000180r 2               ;F9E8 SPI_INIT_DELAY
000180r 2               ;F9CC SPI_RECV
000180r 2               ;F9AD SPI_SEND
000180r 2               ;F9A3 SPI_OPERATION_DONE
000180r 2               ;F974 SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000180r 2               
000180r 2               INROM = $A000
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;       Look closely at hyforth.cfg and the output of ca65/ld65 after
000180r 2               ;  a build; the RAM and ROM code is carefully arranged when the binary
000180r 2               ;  is created, to make initialization easier and optimize RAM use.
000180r 2               ;  The main program engine starts at $EA00 in OS ROM and is only about 500 bytes
000180r 2               ;  long.  Additional functions, then initialization and support code follow.
000180r 2               ;  The heap or 'dictionary' primitives are stored on a banked ROM, mapped
000180r 2               ;  to $A000, along with some meta-compiled words following the primitives.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;       Currently, the primitives are loaded with 'COPYTORAM' (at $A000)
000180r 2               ;  using parameters for the size and location of the heap stored at $A003,4,5.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;       The dictionary has a particular structure, with 'bye' and 'abort'
000180r 2               ;  at the beginning; there are some core words, then contents of 'primitives.s',
000180r 2               ; then 'hywords.s', followed by the end of the dictionary with critical items
000180r 2               ; such as 'fetch', 'store', 'immediate', 'compile, 'semis', 'exit, and ancillary
000180r 2               ; stuff.  Any sub-routines that are called by multiple words (and therefore need set
000180r 2               ; addresses) appear in the 'utes.s' or 'upper.s' files.  These are considered
000180r 2               ; 'support code' and reside in OS ROM following the basic HyForth engine.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;       HyForth is started by running 'A00R' or 'EC00R' from Wozmon;
000180r 2               ;  the basic functions can be supplemented with the meta-compiled words, or
000180r 2               ;  'libraries' as well as runtime-loaded binaries and other items.  Libraries
000180r 2               ;  are loaded with 'autoload' or 'bload' (compiled or binary).
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;                   ZERO PAGE USAGE
000180r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               .segment "ZEROPAGE"
00002Dr 2               ;
00002Dr 2               .org $A0
0000A0  2               ZPSTART:
0000A0  2                                ;   16 bytes for PAD and clipboard
0000A0  2                                ;
0000A0  2  xx xx        PADPTR:     .res 2             ; pointer to top of PAD linked list     $A0
0000A2  2  00           DELIM:      .byte 0            ; default string delimiter       $A2
0000A3  2  00           FLAGS:      .byte 0            ; all purpose flags byte         $A3
0000A4  2  00 00        TEMPA:      .word 0            ; TEMP var A                     $A4
0000A6  2  00 00        TEMPB:      .word 0            ; TEMP var B                     $A6
0000A8  2  00 00        TEMPC:      .word 0            ; TEMP var C                     $A8
0000AA  2  00 00        TEMPD:      .word 0            ; TEMP var D                     $AA
0000AC  2  00 00        TEMPE:      .word 0            ; TEMP var E                     $AC
0000AE  2  00 00        TEMPF:      .word 0            ; TEMP var F                     $AE
0000B0  2               ;
0000B0  2               ;                 Utilities, some BIOS crossover
0000B0  2               ;
0000B0  2  xx xx        Z_DEBUG:    .res 2             ; dedicated ZP temp for debugger  $B0
0000B2  2               ;
0000B2  2  xx xx        Z_MATH1:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math routines   $B2  (*, /, */, felin)
0000B4  2  xx xx        Z_MATH2:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math routines   $B4  (*, /, */, felin)
0000B6  2  xx xx        Z_MATH3:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math routines   $B6  (*, /, */, felin)
0000B8  2  xx xx        Z_MATH4:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math/ANSI       $B8  (HEX2DIG, */)
0000BA  2               ;
0000BA  2  xx xx        Z_ANSI1:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for ANSI            $BA
0000BC  2  xx xx        Z_ANSI2:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for ANSI            $BC
0000BE  2  xx xx        Z_ANSI3:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for ANSI            $BE
0000C0  2               ;
0000C0  2               ;                  HyFedit specific
0000C0  2               ;
0000C0  2  xx xx        HFE_BPTR:   .res 2             ; pointer into 1K buffer      $C0
0000C2  2  xx           HFE_CH:     .res 1             ; cursor horizontal (columns) $C2
0000C3  2  xx           HFE_CV:     .res 1             ; cursor vertical (rows)      $C3
0000C4  2  xx           HFE_T0:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C4
0000C5  2  xx           HFE_T0H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C5
0000C6  2  xx           HFE_T1:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C6
0000C7  2  xx           HFE_T1H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C7
0000C8  2               ;HFE_T2 is at $FE/$FF  (TEMP7)
0000C8  2               ;
0000C8  2               ;                   HyForth setup stuff
0000C8  2               ;
0000C8  2               TEMP0:                    ; DUMPREG, lib/libs    $C8
0000C8  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CA  2               TEMP8:                        ;  cons and pick   $CA
0000CA  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CC  2               TEMP9:                        ;   Imm            $CC
0000CC  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CE  2               MEMPTR:                       ;   malloc         $CE
0000CE  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000D0  2               MEMLAST:
0000D0  2  xx xx           .res 2                      ;  malloc         $D0
0000D2  2               TIB:
0000D2  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; pointer to input buffer     $D2
0000D4  2               TIBEND:
0000D4  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; pointer to end of TIB       $D4
0000D6  2               DFLAG:
0000D6  2  xx              .res 1          ; debug flag                  $D6
0000D7  2               ERRFLAG:
0000D7  2  xx              .res 1          ; error type, 0 = none        $D7
0000D8  2               ERRPTR:
0000D8  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; ptr to mitigation/message   $D8
0000DA  2               DIGBASE:
0000DA  2  xx              .res 1          ; base for number conversion  $DA
0000DB  2               RSEED:
0000DB  2  xx xx xx xx     .res 4          ; random # seed               $DB
0000DF  2               ALFLAG:            ; autoload flag               $DF
0000DF  2  xx              .res 1
0000E0  2               ;
0000E0  2               ;                   internal Forth
0000E0  2               ;   NOTE:  do NOT change the order of the first eight words;
0000E0  2               ;     AGSB's 'training' words assume that STATUS -> WORKREG
0000E0  2               ;     are consecutive and in below sequence.
0000E0  2               ;
0000E0  2  00 00        STATUS:     .word $0   ; state at lsb, last size+flag at msb   $E0
0000E2  2  00 00        CURBUF:     .word $0   ; CURBUF next free byte in TIB          $E2
0000E4  2  00 00        LASTHEAP:   .word $0   ; last link cell                        $E4
0000E6  2  00 00        NEXTHEAP:   .word $0   ; next free cell in heap dictionary     $E6
0000E8  2               ;
0000E8  2               ;                   pointer registers
0000E8  2               ;
0000E8  2  00 00        DSPTR:      .word $0   ; data stack pointer                    $E8
0000EA  2  00 00        RTPTR:      .word $0   ; return stack pointer                  $EA
0000EC  2  00 00        INSTPTR:    .word $0   ; instruction pointer                   $EC
0000EE  2  00 00        WORKREG:    .word $0   ; working register                      $EE
0000F0  2               ;
0000F0  2               ;                    TEMP1 - 4
0000F0  2               ;
0000F0  2  00 00        TEMP1:    .word $0     ; first                                 $F0
0000F2  2  00 00        TEMP2:    .word $0     ; second                                $F2
0000F4  2  00 00        TEMP3:    .word $0     ; third  (two bytes)                    $F4
0000F6  2  00 00        TEMP4:    .word $0     ; fourth  (two bytes)                   $F6
0000F8  2               ;
0000F8  2               ;          NXTTOK, BACKHEAP, TEMP5 - 7
0000F8  2               ;
0000F8  2  00 00        NXTTOK:     .word $0   ; next token in tib (INBUF)             $F8
0000FA  2  00 00        BACKHEAP:   .word $0   ; hold 'here while compile              $FA
0000FC  2               supprint:              ; suppress printing in MEMCPY
0000FC  2  xx           TEMP5:   .res 1        ;    WORDS, supprint for MEMCPY         $FC
0000FD  2  xx           TEMP6:   .res 1        ;    WORDS, DIGCOUNT, TEXTGET           $FD
0000FE  2               HFE_T2:                ;   third temp variable for HyFedit
0000FE  2  xx xx        TEMP7:   .res 2        ;    AUTOLOAD, HyFedit                  $FE
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ; *** $B0-$FF total usage in ZP, including TEMP vars ***
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ;   HYDRA-16 serial/read buffers at $200 and $300 so skip those
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000100  2               ;                   FORTH STACKS
000100  2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000100  2               .segment "BUFFERS"
000200r 2               INBUF:                                      ;    $400
000200r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 256
000204r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000208r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000300r 2               DS:                          ; data stack (S)    $500
000300r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000304r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000308r 2  xx xx xx xx  
00037Er 2  xx xx              .res 2
000380r 2               RT:                          ; return stack (R)  $580
000380r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000384r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000388r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0003FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000400r 2               MEMSTK:                      ; memory manager    $600
000400r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 510
000404r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000408r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0005FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000600r 2               PAD:                         ; pad memory         $800
000600r 2               ;     .res 256
000600r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 512               ; clipboard / command history (together with PAD for now)
000604r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000608r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000800r 2               ;CLIP:
000800r 2               ;      .res 256
000800r 2               ;
000800r 2               ;
000800r 2               .segment "FORTH_ROM"              ; regular core $EC00 and RAM segment at $0A00 now
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; ************ the real deal...
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               
000000r 2               forth_main:
000000r 2  4C rr rr         jmp F_cold
000003r 2  4C rr rr         jmp COPYTORAM
000006r 2               HYPROMPT:
000006r 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
000008r 2  48 46 3E         .byte "HF>"
00000Br 2  00               .byte 0
00000Cr 2               ;
00000Cr 2               HYLIBMSG:
00000Cr 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
00000Er 2  4C 69 62 61      .byte "Libaries:"
000012r 2  72 69 65 73  
000016r 2  3A           
000017r 2  00               .byte 0
000018r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
000018r 2               WDISP:
000018r 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
00001Ar 2  57 3D            .byte "W="
00001Cr 2  00               .byte 0
00001Dr 2               WATDISP:
00001Dr 2  20 20 5B 57      .byte "  [W]="
000021r 2  5D 3D        
000023r 2  00               .byte 0
000024r 2               .endif
000024r 2               ;
000024r 2               ;
000024r 2               ;
000024r 2               F_cold:                         ; cold start - clears EVERYTHING
000024r 2  D8               cld
000025r 2  20 rr rr         jsr CLEAR                   ; zero out zero page, INBUF, DS, RT, SCRBUF
000028r 2                                               ;
000028r 2                                               ; initialize HEAP pointers
000028r 2  AD 03 A0         lda INROM+3                 ; standard 'heap' has been copied to RAM
00002Br 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1            ; and 'switcher' ('exit') code handles
00002Dr 2                                               ; the rest, with 'nest', 'unnest', 'keeps' 'next', 'jump'
00002Dr 2                                               ; 'spull', 'spush', 'comma' etc. all in ROM
00002Dr 2  AD 04 A0         lda INROM+4                 ; wherever THAT ends up.
000030r 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP
000032r 2                                               ; next heap free cell
000032r 2  AD 05 A0         lda INROM+5                 ; along with 'ends'
000035r 2  1A               inc a
000036r 2  85 E7            sta NEXTHEAP + 1
000038r 2  64 E6            stz NEXTHEAP
00003Ar 2  64 D7            stz ERRFLAG                ; clear ERROR flag
00003Cr 2  64 A2            stz DELIM                  ; clear string/search delimiter
00003Er 2  64 A3            stz FLAGS                  ; HF flags register
000040r 2               
000040r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>(MEMSTK+MEMEND)       ; initialize memory manager area
000042r 2  84 CF            sty MEMPTR + 1
000044r 2  A0 rr            ldy #<(MEMSTK+MEMEND)
000046r 2  84 CE            sty MEMPTR
000048r 2  A0 F0            ldy #<MEMTOP
00004Ar 2  84 D0            sty MEMLAST
00004Cr 2  A0 7B            ldy #>MEMTOP
00004Er 2  84 D1            sty MEMLAST+1
000050r 2               
000050r 2  A0 00            ldy #<SCRBUF                ; initialize screen pointer
000052r 2  8C rr rr         sty HFEBUF                  ; HFE_BPTR is set up when
000055r 2  A0 7C            ldy #>SCRBUF                ; HFE_COLD is called
000057r 2  8C rr rr         sty HFEBUF+1                ; done.  SCRBUF already cleared
00005Ar 2               
00005Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00005Ar 2               ; various reinitialization points
00005Ar 2               ;
00005Ar 2               F_warm:                          ; warm start.  Clears TIB, DS, RT, and PAD
00005Ar 2               reset:                           ; leaves heap, memory area, and screen buffer alone
00005Ar 2  A0 FD            ldy #INBUF_end
00005Cr 2  84 D4            sty TIBEND
00005Er 2  A0 rr            ldy #<INBUF
000060r 2  84 D2            sty TIB
000062r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>INBUF
000064r 2  84 D3            sty TIB+1
000066r 2  84 D5            sty TIBEND+1
000068r 2  84 E3            sty CURBUF+1
00006Ar 2  84 F9            sty NXTTOK+1
00006Cr 2               
00006Cr 2  A0 rr            ldy #>DS                     ; DS and RT are now half page each
00006Er 2  84 E9            sty DSPTR + 1
000070r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>RT
000072r 2  84 EB            sty RTPTR + 1
000074r 2               
000074r 2  20 rr rr         jsr PADINIT                  ; clear PAD
000077r 2               
000077r 2  A9 FF            lda #$FF                     ; DEBUG OFF by default
000079r 2  85 D6            sta DFLAG
00007Br 2               ;
00007Br 2  A9 rr            lda #<STARTAUTO                ; check to see if anything to autoload
00007Dr 2  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00007Fr 2  A9 rr            lda #>STARTAUTO
000081r 2  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000083r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000085r 2  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000087r 2  F0 0B            beq warm_next
000089r 2               
000089r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS                   ; status is 'interpret' == \0
00008Br 2  64 DF            stz ALFLAG
00008Dr 2  A0 7E            ldy #<DSEND
00008Fr 2  84 E8            sty DSPTR
000091r 2                   ;
000091r 2                   ;  stz DFLAG           ; start debug
000091r 2                   ;
000091r 2  4C rr rr         jmp CLOAD_ENTER
000094r 2               ;
000094r 2               warm_next:
000094r 2  64 DF            stz ALFLAG                   ; autoload flag OFF
000096r 2               
000096r 2               abort:                            ; clear DS (clear data stack)
000096r 2  A0 7E            ldy #<DSEND
000098r 2  84 E8            sty DSPTR
00009Ar 2               
00009Ar 2               errrtn:                          ; return from error w/o clearing DS
00009Ar 2               quit:
00009Ar 2  A0 FE            ldy #<RTEND                  ; clear RT
00009Cr 2  84 EA            sty RTPTR
00009Er 2               
00009Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr WERROR                   ; print any error messages, clear error flags
0000A1r 2  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; reset INBUF
0000A3r 2  A9 00            lda #0
0000A5r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB),y                  ; clear INBUF stuff
0000A7r 2  64 E2            stz CURBUF                   ; clear cursor  (pointer into INBUF)
0000A9r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS                   ; status is 'interpret' == \0
0000ABr 2               
0000ABr 2  2C               .byte $2c       ; mask next two bytes, jump to resolve/okey
0000ACr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000ACr 2               ; the outer loop
0000ACr 2               
0000ACr 2               resolvept:
0000ACr 2  rr rr            .word okey
0000AEr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000AEr 2               okey:               ; well shit, I hope this is easy....
0000AEr 2               resolve:           ; get a token
0000AEr 2  20 rr rr         jsr token      ; then just process the regular way
0000B1r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
0000B1r 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG                 ; DEBUG
0000B3r 2  D0 0F            bne RVPSKIP
0000B5r 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
0000B9r 2  rr A9 0A 20  
0000BDr 2  rr rr        
0000BFr 2  A9 50            lda #'P'
0000C1r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR            ; DEBUG
0000C4r 2               .endif
0000C4r 2               
0000C4r 2               RVPSKIP:
0000C4r 2               
0000C4r 2               RESFIND:                ; load last or 'latest' word on heap
0000C4r 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP + 1
0000C6r 2  85 F3            sta TEMP2 + 1
0000C8r 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP
0000CAr 2  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0000CCr 2               
0000CCr 2               RESLOOP:              ; lsb linked list
0000CCr 2  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0000CEr 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG             ; so 'last' -> W
0000D0r 2  05 F3            ora TEMP2+1             ; only zero if both are zero
0000D2r 2  D0 07            bne RESEACH              ; PGS - did he forget this?  Sure did...
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2               WORDNOTFOUND:
0000D4r 2  A9 05            lda #ERR_UKW           ; UNKNOWN WORD error
0000D6r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0000D8r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn ; end of dictionary, no more words to search, abort
0000DBr 2               
0000DBr 2               RESEACH:                        ; msb linked list
0000DBr 2  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1
0000DDr 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1           ; update next link
0000DFr 2               
0000DFr 2  A2 EE            ldx #WORKREG
0000E1r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
0000E3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                  ; W += 2, now pointing at size/flag byte from 'here'
0000E6r 2  A0 00            ldy #0              ; compare words
0000E8r 2  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG), y    ; save the flag, first byte is (size and flag)
0000EAr 2  85 E1            sta STATUS + 1
0000ECr 2                           ;; *** start of mod for bit check
0000ECr 2  29 3F            and #$3F            ; mask off flags
0000EEr 2  38               sec
0000EFr 2  F1 F8            sbc (NXTTOK), y    ; compare lengths
0000F1r 2  D0 D9            bne RESLOOP
0000F3r 2  C8               iny
0000F4r 2               ; compare chars
0000F4r 2               RESEQUAL:
0000F4r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
0000F6r 2  C9 20            cmp #$20            ; space ends
0000F8r 2  F0 09            beq RESDONE
0000FAr 2  38               sec                 ; verify
0000FBr 2  F1 EE            sbc (WORKREG), y
0000FDr 2  0A               asl                 ; clean 7-bit ascii
0000FEr 2  D0 CC            bne RESLOOP
000100r 2  C8               iny                 ; get next char
000101r 2  D0 F1            bne RESEQUAL        ;  essentially 'bra' unless y is incremented past $FF
000103r 2               
000103r 2               RESDONE:
000103r 2  98               tya                ; increment W by y, W will point at CFA?
000104r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
000107r 2               
000107r 2               eval:
000107r 2               ; executing ? if status = 0
000107r 2  A5 E0            lda STATUS
000109r 2  F0 1F            beq execute
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               ; falls thru on compile, but...
00010Br 2               ; immediate ? if status+1 < 0 (bit seven set)
00010Br 2  A5 E1            lda STATUS + 1
00010Dr 2  30 1B            bmi immediate
00010Fr 2               
00010Fr 2               compile:          ; otherwise compile
00010Fr 2               .ifdef DEBUG
00010Fr 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG          ; DEBUG, print C if here
000111r 2  D0 0F            bne CMPSKIP
000113r 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
000117r 2  rr A9 0A 20  
00011Br 2  rr rr        
00011Dr 2  A9 43            lda #'C'
00011Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000122r 2               CMPSKIP:
000122r 2               .endif
000122r 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma          ; copy W into NEXTHEAP ('here'), increment NEXTHEAP
000125r 2  B0 03            bcs immediate
000127r 2  4C rr rr         jmp resolve         ; if not 'immediate' go on to next token
00012Ar 2               ;
00012Ar 2               immediate:
00012Ar 2               execute:
00012Ar 2               
00012Ar 2               .ifdef DEBUG
00012Ar 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG         ; DEBUG, print E if here
00012Cr 2  D0 0F            bne EXESKIP
00012Er 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
000132r 2  rr A9 0A 20  
000136r 2  rr rr        
000138r 2  A9 45            lda #'E'
00013Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00013Dr 2               EXESKIP:
00013Dr 2               .endif
00013Dr 2  A9 rr            lda #>resolvept     ; set up INSTPTR to run,
00013Fr 2  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1     ; or return to interpreter.
000141r 2  A9 rr            lda #<resolvept
000143r 2  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
000145r 2  4C rr rr         jmp pick             ; almost done, 'next' and either ENTER or EXEC
000148r 2               
000148r 2               ;-----------------------START PROCESSING INPUT-----------------------
000148r 2               try:
000148r 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y                   ; index is in y
00014Ar 2  F0 04            beq getline    ; if \0  - get a line if pointing at 0
00014Cr 2  C8               iny
00014Dr 2  49 20            eor #$20    ; return 0 in  A if a space
00014Fr 2  60               rts
000150r 2               
000150r 2               ;--------------------GET AN INPUT LINE ENDING WITH CR/LF ------------
000150r 2               getline:   ; drop rts of try, fall through to 'token'
000150r 2  68               pla
000151r 2  68               pla
000152r 2               ;
000152r 2               ;   DO AUTOLOAD HERE
000152r 2               ;      load a space, then copy next line to buffer
000152r 2               ;      calc y (length + 1) jump to GETLNEND
000152r 2               ;
000152r 2  A5 DF            lda ALFLAG
000154r 2  F0 06            beq GLNORMAL
000156r 2  20 rr rr         jsr ALOADTIB
000159r 2  4C rr rr         jmp GETLNSKIPCRLF
00015Cr 2               
00015Cr 2               GLNORMAL:
00015Cr 2  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw HYPROMPT    ; print prompt
000160r 2  rr rr F0 06  
000164r 2  C8 20 rr rr  
00016Br 2               ;
00016Br 2  A0 00            ldy #0   ; leave the first
00016Dr 2               GETLOOP:
00016Dr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; dummy store on first pass, overwritten
00016Fr 2  C8               iny
000170r 2  C4 D4            cpy TIBEND
000172r 2  F0 44            beq GETLNEND
000174r 2  C0 FF            cpy #$FF
000176r 2  D0 02            bne GETREADLOOP
000178r 2  A0 01            ldy #1
00017Ar 2               GETREADLOOP:            ; character read loop
00017Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr READ_CHAR_NE    ; read with no echo, so we can write back what we want
00017Dr 2  90 FB            bcc GETREADLOOP
00017Fr 2                   ;
00017Fr 2                   ;  will check here for ctrl-a,c,v,x
00017Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr CTRLCHECK
000182r 2  B0 F6            bcs GETREADLOOP
000184r 2                   ;
000184r 2  C9 09            cmp #$09         ; handle tab
000186r 2  D0 1F            bne GETLNNOTAB
000188r 2  48 A9 0D 20      WCRLF             ; yup, was a tab
00018Cr 2  rr rr A9 0A  
000190r 2  20 rr rr 68  
000194r 2  A9 20            lda #$20          ; CRLF and indent
000196r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000199r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Cr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0001A2r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0001A5r 2  80 C6            bra GETLOOP      ; will store a space rather than tab
0001A7r 2               GETLNNOTAB:
0001A7r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR    ; just write back what we got
0001AAr 2  C9 0D            cmp #$0D
0001ACr 2  F0 0A            beq GETLNEND
0001AEr 2  C9 08            cmp #$08         ; handle backspace
0001B0r 2  D0 BB            bne GETLOOP
0001B2r 2  88               dey              ; yup, backing up
0001B3r 2  88               dey
0001B4r 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y      ; make sure prev char not overwritten
0001B6r 2  80 B5            bra GETLOOP
0001B8r 2               GETLNEND:                ; clear all if y eq \0
0001B8r 2  48 A9 0D 20      WCRLF                 ; macro, preserves A
0001BCr 2  rr rr A9 0A  
0001C0r 2  20 rr rr 68  
0001C4r 2               GETLNSKIPCRLF:          ; SKIP to here if don't want CRLF
0001C4r 2  A9 20            lda #$20
0001C6r 2  5A               phy
0001C7r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001C9r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y       ; start with space
0001CBr 2  7A               ply
0001CCr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y        ; ends with space
0001CEr 2  A9 00            lda #0            ; mark eol with 0
0001D0r 2  C8               iny
0001D1r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y
0001D3r 2  88               dey
0001D4r 2               ; start it
0001D4r 2  85 E2            sta CURBUF
0001D6r 2               
0001D6r 2               ;------------------AWKWARD Brazilian english comment here...-----------
0001D6r 2               ;
0001D6r 2               ;    **** docs for 'token', the linchpin of the parser ***
0001D6r 2               ;
0001D6r 2               ; in place every token,
0001D6r 2               ; the counter is placed at last space before word
0001D6r 2               ; no rewinds
0001D6r 2               ;
0001D6r 2               ;  (i.e 'the length byte goes immediately before name of word')
0001D6r 2               ;
0001D6r 2               ;    to access the buffer after a line is entered, to something like...
0001D6r 2               ;
0001D6r 2               ;      jsr token
0001D6r 2               ;      ldy #0
0001D6r 2               ;      lda (NXTTOK), y       ; length will be in A
0001D6r 2               ;      .... do some shit ...
0001D6r 2               ;loop:
0001D6r 2               ;      iny
0001D6r 2               ;      lda (NXTTOK), y       ; first char of token
0001D6r 2               ;      cmp #$20
0001D6r 2               ;      beq done             ; if is space, token done
0001D6r 2               ;      ... do some shit ...
0001D6r 2               ;      bra loop             ; go get next char
0001D6r 2               ;      ... etc ....
0001D6r 2               ;done:
0001D6r 2               ;
0001D6r 2               token:
0001D6r 2  A4 E2            ldy CURBUF   ; last position on INBUF
0001D8r 2               
0001D8r 2               TOKENSKIP:   ; skip spaces
0001D8r 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001DBr 2  F0 FB            beq TOKENSKIP
0001DDr 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <start of input word> + 1
0001DEr 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001E0r 2               
0001E0r 2               TOKENSCAN:  ; scan spaces
0001E0r 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001E3r 2  D0 FB            bne TOKENSCAN
0001E5r 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <end of input word> + 1
0001E6r 2  84 E2            sty CURBUF
0001E8r 2               
0001E8r 2               TOKENDONE:  ; find size and store it;
0001E8r 2  98               tya
0001E9r 2  38               sec
0001EAr 2  E5 F8            sbc NXTTOK
0001ECr 2  A4 F8            ldy NXTTOK    ; keep it
0001EEr 2  88               dey
0001EFr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; store size for counted string
0001F1r 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001F3r 2                   ;
0001F3r 2                   ;  During interpretive mode at least...do number and string capture here, before
0001F3r 2                   ;     looking at word list; will be pushed on stack.
0001F3r 2                   ;  This SHOULD have a general digit converter (any base up to 16); return C = 1 if no conversion
0001F3r 2                   ;   if SINGLE is defined, this will skip single digit numbers and use hardcoded ones.
0001F3r 2                   ;
0001F3r 2                   ;  Following check for #'s, check for quoted inline txt a la 'q^....^'
0001F3r 2                   ;
0001F3r 2               .ifdef numbers
0001F3r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001F5r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0001F7r 2  AA               tax                ; store size in X, pass to conversion
0001F8r 2  20 rr rr         jsr DIGCONVT
0001FBr 2  B0 02            bcs CHKFERTXT       ; if some error in conversion, skip and continue processing
0001FDr 2  80 0C            bra TOKCLR0
0001FFr 2               .endif  ; 'numbers'
0001FFr 2               CHKFERTXT:
0001FFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr TXG3            ; TEXTGET - new version, WORKS!
000202r 2  B0 18            bcs TOKENEND
000204r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000206r 2  A9 20            lda #$20
000208r 2  4C rr rr         jmp TOKCLR
00020Br 2               TOKCLR0:
00020Br 2  A0 00            ldy #0
00020Dr 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y     ; load length again
00020Fr 2  AA               tax
000210r 2  E8               inx
000211r 2  A9 20            lda #$20         ; copy spaces over entire converted string
000213r 2               TOKCLR:
000213r 2  91 F8            sta (NXTTOK),y
000215r 2  C8               iny
000216r 2  CA               dex
000217r 2  D0 FA            bne TOKCLR
000219r 2               TOKNEXT:
000219r 2  4C rr rr         jmp token               ; and use 'token' to rebuild input buffer w/o converted #
00021Cr 2               
00021Cr 2               
00021Cr 2               TOKENEND:
00021Cr 2  18               clc     ; clean - setup token
00021Dr 2  60               rts
00021Er 2               ;
00021Er 2               ;--------------------UTILITIES----------------------------------------
00021Er 2               ;    A whole bunch of helper functions;
00021Er 2               ;  wcomma / comma increment WORKREG (or another reg) and NEXTHEAP ('here')
00021Er 2               ;  while copying into NEXTHEAP, uses incwx.
00021Er 2               ;
00021Er 2               ;  copyinto / copyfrom copy AND increment/decrement
00021Er 2               ;  spush/rpush - pushes something onto stacks indexed on ZP by Y (increment using addwx/incwx)
00021Er 2               ;  spull/rpull - pulls from stacks, copies into ZP indexed by Y (decrement builtin)
00021Er 2               ;
00021Er 2               ;  addwx/incwx/decwx do some of the inc/dec-rementing duties
00021Er 2               ;
00021Er 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00021Er 2               ;
00021Er 2               ;   COMMA allocates memory at top of heap for 'other stuff'
00021Er 2               ;   and makes sure heap and working reg pointers are updated.
00021Er 2               ;
00021Er 2               ; heap linked list (moves forward)
00021Er 2               ;
00021Er 2               wcomma:
00021Er 2  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG                  ; copy addr at WORKREG, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
000220r 2               comma:
000220r 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                 ; Y has source of address, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
000222r 2                   ; FALL THROUGH - copyinto, then rts after second incwx
000222r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000222r 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
000222r 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000222r 2               ;
000222r 2               copyinto:
000222r 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
000225r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000227r 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx
00022Ar 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
00022Dr 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
00022Fr 2  4C rr rr         jmp incwx                        ; incwx ends with rts!
000232r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000232r 2               ;
000232r 2               ; generics - PUSH, PULL, incwx/addwx, copyfrom
000232r 2               ;
000232r 2               ;------------------------PUSH a cell--------------------------------
000232r 2               spush_2:
000232r 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3       ; push TEMP3 on top
000234r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
000237r 2               spush_1:
000237r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2       ; push TEMP2 on top
000239r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
00023Cr 2               spush_0:             ; push TEMP1 to stack, probably top of stack
00023Cr 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1
00023Er 2                    ; FALL THROUGH
00023Er 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00023Er 2               ; PUSH a cell
00023Er 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
00023Er 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
00023Er 2               spush:
00023Er 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
000240r 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
000242r 2  C9 rr            cmp #<DS
000244r 2  F0 20            beq ptrerr_s            ; DS boundary check
000246r 2  4C rr rr         jmp push
000249r 2               rpush:
000249r 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
00024Br 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR
00024Dr 2  C9 rr            cmp #<RT                ; RT boundary check
00024Fr 2  F0 11            beq ptrerr_r
000251r 2               
000251r 2                   ; FALL THROUGH
000251r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000251r 2               ; classic stack backwards
000251r 2               push:
000251r 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
000254r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000257r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000259r 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
00025Cr 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
00025Fr 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000261r 2  60               rts
000262r 2               ;
000262r 2               ;                      pointer error (DS or RT)
000262r 2               ptrerr_r:                      ; pop jsr off stack, throw error
000262r 2  A9 01            lda #ERR_RPTR
000264r 2  80 02            bra ptrerr_cont
000266r 2               ptrerr_s:
000266r 2  A9 02            lda #ERR_SPTR
000268r 2               ptrerr_cont:
000268r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
00026Ar 2  68               pla
00026Br 2  68               pla
00026Cr 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
00026Fr 2               ;
00026Fr 2               ;---------------- PULL a cell, with convenience for TEMP 1/2/3 -----
00026Fr 2               ;
00026Fr 2               spull_2:
00026Fr 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3              ; pull TEMP3 from top
000271r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
000274r 2               
000274r 2               spull_1:                    ; pull TEMP2 from top
000274r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
000276r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
000279r 2               
000279r 2               spull_0:
000279r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1             ; pull TEMP1 from top of DS
00027Br 2               ;
00027Br 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
00027Br 2               ;
00027Br 2               ; PULL a cell
00027Br 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
00027Br 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
00027Br 2               ;
00027Br 2               spull:
00027Br 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
00027Dr 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR         ; pointer bounds checking
00027Fr 2  C9 7E            cmp #DSEND        ; ditto
000281r 2  F0 E3            beq ptrerr_s      ; ditto
000283r 2  4C rr rr         jmp pull          ; pull includes rts from incwx so....
000286r 2               rpull:                ;
000286r 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
000288r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR        ; pointer bounds checking
00028Ar 2  C9 FE            cmp #RTEND        ; ditto
00028Cr 2  F0 D4            beq ptrerr_r        ; ditto
00028Er 2               ;
00028Er 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
00028Er 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00028Er 2               ;
00028Er 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
00028Er 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
00028Er 2               pull:
00028Er 2               copyfrom:
00028Er 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
000290r 2  99 00 00         sta 0, y
000293r 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx      ; NOTE:  not a jmp, returns here.
000296r 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
000298r 2  99 01 00         sta 1, y
00029Br 2                ;
00029Br 2                ;  FALL THROUGH
00029Br 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00029Br 2               ; increment a word in page zero, identified by X and offset to it from $00.
00029Br 2               ;
00029Br 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr incwx/addwx/decwx
00029Br 2               ;
00029Br 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
00029Br 2               ;   zeropage pointers.  Offsets indexed by
00029Br 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
00029Br 2               ;   pointer location; X IDENTIFIES the 16-bit pointer in
00029Br 2               ;   question, period.
00029Br 2               ;
00029Br 2               incwx:
00029Br 2  A9 01            lda #01
00029Dr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00029Dr 2               ; add a byte in A to a word in page zero. X identifies the pointer
00029Dr 2               ; being incremented, and is not itself dec'd or inc'd.
00029Dr 2               ;
00029Dr 2               addwx:
00029Dr 2  18               clc
00029Er 2  75 00            adc 0, x
0002A0r 2  95 00            sta 0, x
0002A2r 2  90 03            bcc addwx_end
0002A4r 2  F6 01            inc 1, x
0002A6r 2  18               clc      ; keep carry clean.
0002A7r 2                            ; our convention is that functions SET the carry for positive results,
0002A7r 2                            ; clear carry for negative or neutral ones.
0002A7r 2               addwx_end:
0002A7r 2  60               rts
0002A8r 2               
0002A8r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0002A8r 2               ;          decwx, heap moves
0002A8r 2               ; decrement a word in page zero, identified by X and offset to it from $00.
0002A8r 2               ;
0002A8r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr decwx
0002A8r 2               ;
0002A8r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
0002A8r 2               ;   zeropage pointers.  Offsets indexed by
0002A8r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
0002A8r 2               ;   pointer location; X IDENTIFIES the 16-bit pointer in
0002A8r 2               ;   question, period.
0002A8r 2               ;
0002A8r 2               decwx:
0002A8r 2  B5 00            lda 0, x
0002AAr 2  D0 02            bne decwx_end
0002ACr 2  D6 01            dec 1, x
0002AEr 2               decwx_end:
0002AEr 2  D6 00            dec 0, x
0002B0r 2  60               rts
0002B1r 2               
0002B1r 2               ;
0002B1r 2               ;
0002B1r 2               ENGINEEND:
0002B1r 2               ;                           END OF CORE ENGINE
0002B1r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------

00037Br 3

00037Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpull                       ; IP = [RTPTR], RTPTR += 2
000380r 3

000380r 3                                                   ; or first entry in CFA of next word.

000380r 3                                                   ; WORKREG = (INSTPTR) ; INSTPTR += 2
000380r 3  A2 EC            ldx #INSTPTR
000382r 3  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG

000387r 3

000387r 3               .ifdef DEBUG                        ;  DEBUG

000389r 3  D0 2F            bne PRINTWSKIP                  ; print W and [W] etc if DEBUG

00038Fr 3  rr rr F0 06  
000393r 3  C8 20 rr rr  
00039Ar 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG+1
00039Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
00039Fr 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG
0003A1r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
0003A4r 3  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw WATDISP

0003BAr 3               ;
0003BAr 3               ; .. use old-fashioned way AND new tricks
0003BAr 3               ;  ( otherwise known as THE SWITCH )
0003BAr 3               ;
0003BAr 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1                 ; compare pages (MSBs)
0003BCr 3  C9 rr            cmp #>ends + 1                  ;  !! Compiled must be higher in memory than hardcoded !! (see below)
0003BEr 3  30 16            bmi jump                        ; jump over nest if native code
0003C0r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; ldy #0 if check extra byte ($89 or $24 for various 'bit' variants)
0003C2r 3  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG),y
0003C4r 3  F0 10            beq jump                        ; yep, jump to native execution
0003C6r 3               
0003C6r 3               nest:                               ; ENTER in classic Forth lingo   ( COMPILED )
0003C6r 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
0003C8r 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpush                       ; RTPTR -=2, [RTPTR] = [IP]
0003CBr 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG                     ; W = IP
0003CDr 3  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
0003CFr 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1
0003D1r 3  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1
0003D3r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next                        ; next
0003D6r 3               
0003D6r 3               jump:                               ; EXECUTE                      ( PRIMITIVE )
0003D6r 3  6C EE 00         jmp (WORKREG)                   ; start running code at next word
0003D9r 3                                                   ;  JUMP [W]

000414r 3               NEWPAD:                            ; new PAD item setup

000414r 3  A5 A0            lda PADPTR
000416r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000418r 3  A5 A1            lda PADPTR+1                   ; backup PADPTR to TEMP1
00041Ar 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1

00044Br 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
00044Dr 3  38               sec

000450r 3  85 D0            sta MEMLAST
000452r 3  B0 02            bcs MALSK00

000456r 3               MALSK00:
000456r 3  38               sec
000457r 3  E5 F0            sbc TEMP1

00045Br 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1
00045Dr 3  E5 F1            sbc TEMP1+1
00045Fr 3  85 D1            sta MEMLAST+1
000461r 3                                       ;MEMLAST updated to start of new record
000461r 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
000463r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000465r 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1
000467r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1         ; use TEMP3 to walk through clearing of memory
000469r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00046Br 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2            ; write type first
00046Dr 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
00046Fr 3  C8               iny
000470r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1            ; LSB length

000479r 3  A2 F4            ldx #TEMP3

00047Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx            ; increment TEMP3 by 3
000480r 3               MALLOOP:

000486r 3  C6 F0            dec TEMP1
000488r 3  D0 0C            bne MALSK02

0004A0r 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
