0004D2r 3               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0004D2r 3               ;                                TEXTGET / TXG3
0004D2r 3               ;   get escaped, delimited text from INBUF, store in temp or permanent memory
0004D2r 3               ;    'token' has set NXTTOK to starting address in INBUF.
0004D2r 3               ;
0004D2r 3               ;  uses TEMP1, TEMP2, TEMP3 (MALLOC only?), TEMP4, TEMP5, TEMP6, x, y
0004D2r 3               ;    TEMP1 starts as length, then as dest address (pushed on stack to end)
0004D2r 3               ;    TEMP2 as record type, then as modified dest address during copy
0004D2r 3               ;    TEMP3 stomped by MALLOC ('Q'), not affect by 'q' option
0004D2r 3               ;    TEMP4 counts esc chars, 4+1 flag for PAD vs memory dest
0004D2r 3               ;    TEMP5 and TEMP6 are bookends of NXTTOK offset
0004D2r 3               ;    y used as index throughout in various read/cpy operations
0004D2r 3               ;    x at end to copy back so parser knows how many chars to blank out if all goes well
0004D2r 3               ;
0004D2r 3               ;   Original TEXTGET code (TXG2) still stored in 11/12/26, 9:41AM version
0004D2r 3               ;
0004D2r 3               ;          copy/escape - translate escaped chars
0004D2r 3               ;
0004D2r 3               ;      will modify this sometime to require and handle escaped
0004D2r 3               ;    two-digit hex values, ie \EE and such.
0004D2r 3               ;
0004D2r 3               ;  NOTE:  if a \0 or \00 is in middle of string, TEXTGET WILL
0004D2r 3               ;    copy it correctly, but...it may not print or copy right
0004D2r 3               ;    with other functions that expect proper z-termination.
0004D2r 3               ;
0004D2r 3               ;  table includes all chars from $20 to $46 inclusive
0004D2r 3               ;    $30-$39 -> trunc'd to $0x, $41-$46 to $0A-$0F
0004D2r 3               ;    $22 is dbl-quote, $1B (\@) is escape char
0004D2r 3               ;
0004D2r 3               TXHEXTBL:
0004D2r 3  20 21            .byte " !"
0004D4r 3  22               .byte $22
0004D5r 3  23 24 25 26      .byte "#$%&'()*+,-./"
0004D9r 3  27 28 29 2A  
0004DDr 3  2B 2C 2D 2E  
0004E2r 3  00 01 02 03      .byte $00, $01, $02, $03, $04, $05, $06, $07, $08, $09
0004E6r 3  04 05 06 07  
0004EAr 3  08 09        
0004ECr 3  3A 3B 3C 3D      .byte ":;<=>?"
0004F0r 3  3E 3F        
0004F2r 3  1B 0A 0B 0C      .byte $1B, $0A, $0B, $0C, $0D, $0E, $0F
0004F6r 3  0D 0E 0F     
0004F9r 3               
0004F9r 3               TXCPYESC:
0004F9r 3  C8               iny
0004FAr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0004FCr 3  C9 20            cmp #$20                    ; is this a single hex digit
0004FEr 3  90 0D            bcc TXCPYSK                 ; or the escape char '\@'?
000500r 3  C9 47            cmp #$47
000502r 3  F0 09            beq TXCPYSK
000504r 3  B0 07            bcs TXCPYSK
000506r 3  38               sec
000507r 3  E9 20            sbc #$20                    ; if so, lookup substitute
000509r 3  AA               tax
00050Ar 3  BD rr rr         lda TXHEXTBL,x
00050Dr 3               TXCPYSK:
00050Dr 3  C6 F2            dec TEMP2                    ; adjust TEMP2 so we are
00050Fr 3  B0 02            bcs TXDECT2SK                ; not printing the extra '\'
000511r 3  C6 F3            dec TEMP2+1
000513r 3               TXDECT2SK:
000513r 3  60               rts
000514r 3               ;
000514r 3               ;  end of TXG2  (new text capture)
000514r 3               ;
000514r 3               ;-------------- TXG3 -  new version of TEXTGET
000514r 3               ;
000514r 3               TXG3:
000514r 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3GETTIB                 ; TEMP1 will have length, TEMP2 dest addr of string
000517r 3                                                 ; TEMP6 has starting index in buffer, TEMP5 end index
000517r 3               ;
000517r 3               TX3TIBDONE:                       ;
000517r 3                   ;                   now we switch between q (PAD) and Q (MALLOC)
000517r 3                   ;
000517r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
000519r 3  C9 51            cmp #'Q'
00051Br 3  F0 14            beq TX3mCONT                ; 'q' or 'Q' ?
00051Dr 3                   ;
00051Dr 3                   ;  if 'q':
00051Dr 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00051Fr 3  A0 02            ldy #2
000521r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y              ; copy length to PAD
000523r 3  A5 A0            lda PADPTR                  ; and then copy ptr location
000525r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000527r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                    ; copy for pushing later
000529r 3  A5 A1            lda PADPTR+1
00052Br 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1
00052Dr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1                  ; copy for pushing later
00052Fr 3                   ;
00052Fr 3  80 0E            bra TX3tCONT
000531r 3                   ;
000531r 3                   ;    if 'Q':
000531r 3               TX3mCONT:
000531r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MALLOC                      ; TEMP1 now has address on mem stack, length already written
000534r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000536r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000538r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
00053Ar 3  C8               iny
00053Br 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y                   ; and make a copy of address for copy
00053Dr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; operation from input buffer below.
00053Fr 3                   ; continue
00053Fr 3               TX3tCONT:
00053Fr 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3STRCPY
000542r 3               TX3STRCOPYDONE:                   ; copy string and translate escaped chars
000542r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
000544r 3  C9 51            cmp #'Q'
000546r 3  F0 1B            beq TX3mCONT2                ; 'q' or 'Q' ?
000548r 3  A0 02            ldy #2                       ; 'q', so finish updating and incrementing PADPTR
00054Ar 3  B1 A0            lda (PADPTR),y               ; get length
00054Cr 3  1A               inc a
00054Dr 3  1A               inc a
00054Er 3  1A               inc a
00054Fr 3  18               clc
000550r 3  65 A0            adc PADPTR
000552r 3  85 A0            sta PADPTR
000554r 3  90 02            bcc TX3tUPD
000556r 3  E6 A1            inc PADPTR+1
000558r 3               TX3tUPD:
000558r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00055Ar 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00055Cr 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
00055Er 3  C8               iny
00055Fr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000561r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
000563r 3               ;
000563r 3               TX3mCONT2:
000563r 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3FIXTIB
000566r 3               TX3NOGOOD:
000566r 3  38               sec                             ; C=1, no text, so process as token
000567r 3               TX3END:
000567r 3  60               rts
000568r 3               ;
000568r 3               ;--
000568r 3               ;
000568r 3               TX3GETTIB:
000568r 3  64 F6            stz TEMP4                       ; to count escape '\' chars
00056Ar 3  64 F7            stz TEMP4+1                     ; flag for PAD vs memory storage
00056Cr 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1                     ; will store length here
00056Er 3  A9 04            lda #$04
000570r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; record type is 'sz'
000572r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
000574r 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6                       ; to save y for later copy
000576r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip len of first token
000578r 3               TX3SKSPC:
000578r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
00057Ar 3  C9 20            cmp #$20                        ; skip leading spaces
00057Cr 3  D0 03            bne TX3SK00
00057Er 3  C8               iny
00057Fr 3  80 F7            bra TX3SKSPC
000581r 3               TX3SK00:
000581r 3  C9 71            cmp #'q'                        ; text will go to PAD
000583r 3  D0 02            bne TX3SK_Q
000585r 3  80 04            bra TX3SK_q
000587r 3               TX3SK_Q:
000587r 3  C9 51            cmp #'Q'                        ; text will got to memory
000589r 3  D0 DB            bne TX3NOGOOD
00058Br 3               TX3SK_q:
00058Br 3               ;    and #$40                        ; mask off q vs Q bits
00058Br 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1                     ; store q or Q
00058Dr 3  C8               iny
00058Er 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000590r 3  C9 5E            cmp #'^'
000592r 3  D0 D2            bne TX3NOGOOD
000594r 3  C8               iny
000595r 3  84 FD            sty TEMP6                       ; TEMP6 stores pos of first char
000597r 3               TX3SCAN:
000597r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y                  ; find delimiting '^'
000599r 3  C9 5C            cmp #'\'
00059Br 3  D0 02            bne TX3SK04
00059Dr 3  E6 F6            inc TEMP4
00059Fr 3               TX3SK04:
00059Fr 3  C9 5E            cmp #'^'
0005A1r 3  D0 08            bne TX3SK03                    ; handle escaped ^ only
0005A3r 3  88               dey                            ; other escaped chars handled when copied to
0005A4r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y                 ; MALLOC'd address (see TX2COPYLOOP below)
0005A6r 3  C8               iny
0005A7r 3  C9 5C            cmp #'\'
0005A9r 3  D0 0B            bne TX3FOUND
0005ABr 3               TX3SK03:
0005ABr 3  C8               iny                             ; get index back after checking for \^
0005ACr 3  98               tya
0005ADr 3  18               clc
0005AEr 3  65 F8            adc NXTTOK
0005B0r 3  C9 64            cmp #MAXSTR
0005B2r 3  B0 B2            bcs TX3NOGOOD
0005B4r 3  80 E1            bra TX3SCAN
0005B6r 3               TX3FOUND:                         ; quoted text found.  Now do some math.
0005B6r 3  84 FC            sty TEMP5                       ; TEMP5 is pos+1 last letter
0005B8r 3  98               tya
0005B9r 3  38               sec
0005BAr 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6                       ; and TEMP6 is where we started, so...
0005BCr 3  38               sec
0005BDr 3  E5 F6            sbc TEMP4                       ; subtract escape (\) chars
0005BFr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                       ; and this should be the length
0005C1r 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1
0005C3r 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3TIBDONE
0005C6r 3               ;
0005C6r 3               ;--
0005C6r 3               ;
0005C6r 3               ;   now for math.  NXTTOK ptr needs to be ref'd with same y
0005C6r 3               ;    as TEMP2, so we need to match them up...and we are hitting
0005C6r 3               ;    the record at +3, so....TEMP6 is start
0005C6r 3               TX3STRCPY:
0005C6r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0005C8r 3  38               sec
0005C9r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
0005CBr 3  B0 02            bcs TX3SK01
0005CDr 3  C6 F3            dec TEMP2+1
0005CFr 3               TX3SK01:
0005CFr 3  18               clc
0005D0r 3  69 03            adc #3
0005D2r 3  90 02            bcc TX3SK02
0005D4r 3  E6 F3            inc TEMP2+1
0005D6r 3               TX3SK02:
0005D6r 3  A4 FD            ldy TEMP6
0005D8r 3               TX3CPYLOOP:
0005D8r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0005DAr 3  C9 5C            cmp #'\'
0005DCr 3  D0 03            bne  TX3CPY0
0005DEr 3  20 rr rr         jsr TXCPYESC                   ; handle escaped chars (translate using table below)
0005E1r 3               TX3CPY0:
0005E1r 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y
0005E3r 3  C8               iny
0005E4r 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
0005E6r 3  D0 F0            bne TX3CPYLOOP
0005E8r 3               ;
0005E8r 3  A9 00            lda #0
0005EAr 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y                   ; put zero on end
0005ECr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push address from mem stack on DS
0005EFr 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3STRCOPYDONE
0005F2r 3               ;
0005F2r 3               ;
0005F2r 3               TX3FIXTIB:
0005F2r 3  A5 FC            lda TEMP5                       ; now get the length of statement in TIB back
0005F4r 3  38               sec                             ; so can return in X
0005F5r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
0005F7r 3  18               clc
0005F8r 3  69 04            adc #4
0005FAr 3  AA               tax                             ; calc and bring back length to X
0005FBr 3  18               clc                             ; C=0 means all good, so tell parser to cleanup buffer
0005FCr 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3END
0005FFr 3               ;
0005FFr 3               ;
0005FFr 3               ;
0005FFr 3               .ifdef numbers
0005FFr 3               ;------------------------
0005FFr 3               ;      CONVERT DIGITS, PUSH on DS
0005FFr 3               
0005FFr 3               DIGCONVT:    ;  Y is index into NXTPTR, X is length
0005FFr 3  64 F0            stz TEMP1
000601r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
000603r 3  64 F2            stz TEMP2                       ; no necc for bin or hex, but
000605r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1                     ; since is convenient....
000607r 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6
000609r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip over length
00060Br 3  A9 0A            lda #10
00060Dr 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
00060Fr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000611r 3  C9 2D            cmp #'-'
000613r 3  F0 0A            beq DIGCONV_MINUS
000615r 3  C9 24            cmp #'$'
000617r 3  F0 12            beq DIGHEX1
000619r 3  C9 25            cmp #'%'
00061Br 3  F0 07            beq DIGBIN1
00061Dr 3  80 12            bra DIG_SNG
00061Fr 3               DIGCONV_MINUS:
00061Fr 3  E6 FD            inc TEMP6
000621r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
000624r 3               DIGBIN1:
000624r 3  A9 02            lda #2
000626r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
000628r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
00062Br 3               DIGHEX1:
00062Br 3  A9 10            lda #16
00062Dr 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
00062Fr 3               DIG_XY:
00062Fr 3  C8               iny
000630r 3  CA               dex
000631r 3               DIG_SNG:
000631r 3                 .ifdef SINGLE
000631r 3  E0 01            cpx #1                          ; skip single digit, handle hard-wired or...
000633r 3  D0 03            bne DIGCONV_LOOP                ; only one digit left, we can handle that elsewhere
000635r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; not really an error, just return and let 'find' to it
000638r 3                 .endif
000638r 3               DIGCONV_LOOP:
000638r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
00063Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr GETDIG                      ; GETDIG just decides whether A is a digit or not (0-9A-F)
00063Dr 3  90 03            bcc DIGCONT0                    ; C=0 means success
00063Fr 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR
000642r 3               DIGCONT0:
000642r 3  48               pha                             ; else save it to add in a bit
000643r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; do first shift
000645r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000647r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE                     ; check base that was set above
000649r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00064Br 3  F0 06            beq DIGDEC                      ; it's DEC, go there
00064Dr 3  C9 02            cmp #2
00064Fr 3  F0 2F            beq DIGBIN                      ; it's BIN, go there
000651r 3  80 1D            bra DIGHEX                      ; Go to hex if others not true
000653r 3               DIGDEC:
000653r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; 2nd shift; upper nybble doesn't end up right
000655r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000657r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1                       ; load results of two shifts
000659r 3  18               clc
00065Ar 3  65 F2            adc TEMP2                       ; add in previous total from last loop
00065Cr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00065Er 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1                     ; and same with second digit, and the carry
000660r 3  65 F3            adc TEMP2+1
000662r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000664r 3               DIGDEC2:
000664r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; final shift
000666r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000668r 3  68               pla                             ; bring back read digit
000669r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10                         ; make sure it's not hex, mostly
00066Br 3  90 18            bcc DIGCONT                     ; jump to continue
00066Dr 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; else throw error
000670r 3               DIGHEX:
000670r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000672r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000674r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000676r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000678r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
00067Ar 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1                     ; shifted three more times
00067Cr 3  68               pla
00067Dr 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONT
000680r 3               DIGBIN:                             ; already did the single shift
000680r 3  68               pla
000681r 3  C9 02            cmp #2
000683r 3  B0 3E            bcs DIGCONV_ERR                 ; fall through to the additon of new digit
000685r 3               DIGCONT:
000685r 3  18               clc                             ; add digit finally
000686r 3  65 F0            adc TEMP1
000688r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00068Ar 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; copy to intermediate result in case another dec digit
00068Cr 3  90 02            bcc DIGCONT2
00068Er 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1                     ; and inc 2nd byte if necc.
000690r 3               DIGCONT2:
000690r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
000692r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
000694r 3  D0 08            bne DIGCONT3
000696r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000698r 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; save it for next round, regardless
00069Ar 3  C9 80            cmp #$80                        ; check to see if > $8000
00069Cr 3  B0 25            bcs DIGCONV_ERR
00069Er 3               DIGCONT3:
00069Er 3  C8               iny
00069Fr 3  CA               dex
0006A0r 3  D0 96            bne DIGCONV_LOOP
0006A2r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
0006A4r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
0006A6r 3  D0 16            bne DIGCONT4
0006A8r 3               DECFINISH:
0006A8r 3  A5 FD            lda TEMP6                       ; check for minus
0006AAr 3  F0 12            beq DIGCONT4
0006ACr 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0006AEr 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
0006B0r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0006B2r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0006B4r 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
0006B6r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0006B8r 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1
0006BAr 3  D0 02            bne DIGCONT4
0006BCr 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
0006BEr 3               DIGCONT4:
0006BEr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push TEMP1 on to stack
0006C1r 3  18               clc
0006C2r 3  60               rts
0006C3r 3               DIGCONV_ERR:
0006C3r 3  38               sec
0006C4r 3  60               rts
0006C5r 3               
0006C5r 3               ;--------------------------
0006C5r 3               GETDIG:                             ; y is index to next char in NXTTOK
0006C5r 3               ;    lda (NXTTOK),y                 ; move to calling code instead, pass in A
0006C5r 3  38               sec
0006C6r 3  E9 30            sbc #'0'
0006C8r 3  90 10            bcc GETDIG_ERR
0006CAr 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
0006CCr 3  90 0A            bcc GETDIG_RTN
0006CEr 3  E9 07            sbc #7
0006D0r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
0006D2r 3  90 06            bcc GETDIG_ERR
0006D4r 3  C9 10            cmp #16
0006D6r 3  B0 02            bcs GETDIG_ERR
0006D8r 3               GETDIG_RTN:                         ; pass carry clear for good digit
0006D8r 3  18               clc
0006D9r 3  60               rts
0006DAr 3               GETDIG_ERR:                         ; pass carry set for no digit
0006DAr 3  38               sec
0006DBr 3  60               rts
0006DCr 3               ;  end of new number conv
0006DCr 3               ;
0006DCr 3               H2NUM:
0006DCr 3  27 10         .byte $27,$10                ; 10000
0006DEr 3  03 E8         .byte $03,$E8                ; 1000
0006E0r 3  00 64         .byte $00,$64                ; 100
0006E2r 3  00 0A         .byte $00,$0A                ; 10
0006E4r 3               HEX2DEC:                            ; uses X, TEMP1, Z_MATH1,2,3,4
0006E4r 3  A5 A0            lda PADPTR
0006E6r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0006E8r 3  A5 A1            lda PADPTR+1
0006EAr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0006ECr 3  A9 00            lda #0
0006EEr 3  85 B2            sta Z_MATH1                   ; flag for first non-zero digit
0006F0r 3  A2 A0            ldx #PADPTR
0006F2r 3  A9 03            lda #3
0006F4r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0006F7r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
0006F9r 3  A2 00            ldx #0
0006FBr 3               H2DDIV10:
0006FBr 3  BD rr rr         lda H2NUM,x
0006FEr 3  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
000700r 3  E8               inx
000701r 3  BD rr rr         lda H2NUM,x
000704r 3  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
000706r 3  E8               inx
000707r 3  64 B8            stz Z_MATH4
000709r 3               H2DLOOP:
000709r 3  A5 B5            lda Z_MATH2+1
00070Br 3  C5 B7            cmp Z_MATH3+1
00070Dr 3  90 19            bcc  H2DSK1
00070Fr 3  D0 06            bne  H2DSK0
000711r 3  A5 B4            lda Z_MATH2
000713r 3  C5 B6            cmp Z_MATH3
000715r 3  90 11            bcc  H2DSK1
000717r 3               H2DSK0:
000717r 3  A5 B4            lda Z_MATH2
000719r 3  38               sec
00071Ar 3  E5 B6            sbc Z_MATH3
00071Cr 3  85 B4            sta Z_MATH2
00071Er 3  A5 B5            lda Z_MATH2+1
000720r 3  E5 B7            sbc Z_MATH3+1
000722r 3  85 B5            sta Z_MATH2+1
000724r 3  E6 B8            inc Z_MATH4
000726r 3  80 E1            bra H2DLOOP
000728r 3               H2DSK1:
000728r 3  A5 B2            lda Z_MATH1
00072Ar 3  D0 06            bne  H2DSK2
00072Cr 3  A5 B8            lda Z_MATH4
00072Er 3  F0 0A            beq  H2DSK3
000730r 3  E6 B2            inc Z_MATH1
000732r 3               H2DSK2:
000732r 3  A5 B8            lda Z_MATH4
000734r 3  18               clc
000735r 3  69 30            adc #'0'
000737r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
000739r 3  C8               iny
00073Ar 3               H2DSK3:
00073Ar 3  E0 08            cpx #8
00073Cr 3  F0 03            beq H2DFIN
00073Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp H2DDIV10                      ; back to top of loop
000741r 3               H2DFIN:
000741r 3  A5 B4            lda Z_MATH2
000743r 3  18               clc
000744r 3  69 30            adc #'0'
000746r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
000748r 3  C8               iny
000749r 3  A9 00            lda #0                           ; write 0 on end of string, oops!
00074Br 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
00074Dr 3  C8               iny
00074Er 3  5A               phy                               ; will pull back to A here in a bit
00074Fr 3  98               tya
000750r 3  18               clc
000751r 3  65 A0            adc PADPTR
000753r 3  85 A0            sta PADPTR
000755r 3  90 02            bcc H2DSK4
000757r 3  E6 A1            inc PADPTR+1
000759r 3               H2DSK4:
000759r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                       ; push address of string
00075Cr 3                                               ;   update length and PADPTR
00075Cr 3  68               pla                               ; pull back pushed Y
00075Dr 3  A0 02            ldy #2
00075Fr 3  91 F0            sta (TEMP1),y                     ; copy length
000761r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000763r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000765r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y                    ; and copy address of prev
000767r 3  C8               iny                               ; entry here before leaving
000768r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00076Ar 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
00076Cr 3  60               rts
00076Dr 3               ;
00076Dr 3               ;
00076Dr 3               .endif    ; numbers
00076Dr 3               ;
00076Dr 3               ;--------------------------------------------------------------
00076Dr 3               ;                     ctrl check  - s, c, x, v
00076Dr 3               ;
00076Dr 3               ;         ctrl-s - copy TIB 0 to y to current screen buffer line
00076Dr 3               ;         ctrl-c - copy TIB 0 to y to PAD
00076Dr 3               ;         ctrl-v - pull last PAD entry and overwrite TIB starting at y (bounds check)
00076Dr 3               ;         ctrl-x - copy TIB 0 to y to PAD, clear TIB
00076Dr 3               ;
00076Dr 3               CTRLCHECK:                   ; A has last key entered, Y is current position in TIB
00076Dr 3  48               pha
00076Er 3  C9 13            cmp #$13      ; ctrl-s
000770r 3  F0 0F            beq DOCTRLS
000772r 3  C9 03            cmp #$03      ; ctrl-c
000774r 3  F0 33            beq JMPCTRLC
000776r 3  C9 16            cmp #$16      ; ctrl-v   - TIBEND should be check when adding into TIB
000778r 3  F0 2C            beq JMPCTRLV
00077Ar 3  C9 18            cmp #$18      ; ctrl-x
00077Cr 3  F0 2E            beq JMPCTRLX
00077Er 3  18               clc             ; fell through, return
00077Fr 3               CTRLEND:
00077Fr 3  68               pla
000780r 3  60               rts
000781r 3               
000781r 3               DOCTRLS:
000781r 3  20 rr rr         jsr TIB2PAD                   ; backs up Y to TEMP5, too.
000784r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000786r 3  B1 A0            lda (PADPTR),y          ; we want PREVIOUS ITEM addr, not current
000788r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1               ; pointer addr
00078Ar 3  C8               iny
00078Br 3  B1 A0            lda (PADPTR),y
00078Dr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00078Fr 3  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
000791r 3               ;    lda #3
000791r 3  A9 04            lda #4                  ; skip leading space
000793r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
000796r 3  A5 C3            lda HFE_CV              ; paste at current row
000798r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
00079Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr FELIN               ; get row address from TEMP2, returns addr to TEMP2
00079Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr FECPY_TOC           ; and at current column
0007A0r 3  A4 FC            ldy TEMP5                     ; restore y
0007A2r 3  38               sec                           ; C=1 means eat keystroke w/o save to buffer or echo
0007A3r 3  4C rr rr         jmp CTRLEND
0007A6r 3               
0007A6r 3               JMPCTRLV:
0007A6r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DOCTRLV
0007A9r 3               JMPCTRLC:
0007A9r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DOCTRLC
0007ACr 3               JMPCTRLX:
0007ACr 3  4C rr rr         jmp DOCTRLX
0007AFr 3               
0007AFr 3               DOCTRLC:
0007AFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr TIB2PAD                   ; backs up Y to TEMP5, too.
0007B2r 3  A4 FC            ldy TEMP5                     ; restore y
0007B4r 3  38               sec                           ; C=1 means eat keystroke w/o save to buffer or echo
0007B5r 3  4C rr rr         jmp CTRLEND
0007B8r 3               
0007B8r 3               DOCTRLX:
0007B8r 3  20 rr rr         jsr TIB2PAD                   ; backs up Y to TEMP5, too.
0007BBr 3  A4 FC            ldy TEMP5
0007BDr 3  A9 20            lda #$20
0007BFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0007C2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0007C5r 3               DOCXBSPC:
0007C5r 3  A9 08            lda #$08            ; backspace twice, space once
0007C7r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0007CAr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0007CDr 3  A9 20            lda #$20
0007CFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0007D2r 3  88               dey
0007D3r 3  D0 F0            bne DOCXBSPC
0007D5r 3  38               sec                  ; C=1 means eat keystroke w/o save to buffer or echo
0007D6r 3  4C rr rr         jmp CTRLEND
0007D9r 3               
0007D9r 3               TIB2PAD:
0007D9r 3  84 FC            sty TEMP5                      ; save y to restore if necc.
0007DBr 3  A5 A0            lda PADPTR
0007DDr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0007DFr 3  A5 A1            lda PADPTR+1                   ; backup PADPTR to TEMP1
0007E1r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0007E3r 3  A0 02            ldy #2
0007E5r 3  A5 FC            lda TEMP5                      ; write the length while we're here
0007E7r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
0007E9r 3  A9 03            lda #3       ; was 2
0007EBr 3  18               clc
0007ECr 3  65 A0            adc PADPTR
0007EEr 3  85 A0            sta PADPTR
0007F0r 3  90 02            bcc TIB2PSK0
0007F2r 3  E6 A1            inc PADPTR+1
0007F4r 3               TIB2PSK0:
0007F4r 3  A0 00            ldy #0        ; was 1
0007F6r 3  A9 20            lda #$20        ; added
0007F8r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y  ; added
0007FAr 3  C8               iny             ; added
0007FBr 3               TIB2PLOOP:
0007FBr 3  B1 D2            lda (TIB),y
0007FDr 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
0007FFr 3  C8               iny
000800r 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
000802r 3  D0 F7            bne  TIB2PLOOP
000804r 3  A9 00            lda #0
000806r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y            ; write zero at end
000808r 3  A5 FC            lda TEMP5
00080Ar 3  1A               inc a                     ; for the zero
00080Br 3  18               clc
00080Cr 3  65 A0            adc PADPTR
00080Er 3  85 A0            sta PADPTR
000810r 3  90 02            bcc  TIB2PSK1
000812r 3  E6 A1            inc PADPTR+1
000814r 3               TIB2PSK1:
000814r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000816r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000818r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
00081Ar 3  C8               iny
00081Br 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00081Dr 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
00081Fr 3               
00081Fr 3  60               rts
000820r 3               
000820r 3               DOCTRLV:
000820r 3  84 FC            sty TEMP5      ; backup Y
000822r 3               
000822r 3  20 rr rr         jsr DUMPREG    ; temp5 is at $FC, and also look at Y
000825r 3               
000825r 3  A5 D3            lda TIB+1
000827r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000829r 3  98               tya
00082Ar 3  3A               dec a          ; y starts at 1 for some reason
00082Br 3  18               clc
00082Cr 3  65 D2            adc TIB
00082Er 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000830r 3  90 02            bcc DOCV_SK0
000832r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
000834r 3               DOCV_SK0:
000834r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000836r 3  B1 A0            lda (PADPTR),y          ; we want PREVIOUS ITEM addr, not current
000838r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2               ; pointer addr
00083Ar 3  C8               iny
00083Br 3  B1 A0            lda (PADPTR),y
00083Dr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1
00083Fr 3  05 F2            ora TEMP2               ; are we at zero?
000841r 3  D0 06            bne  DOCV_SK1
000843r 3  A4 FC            ldy TEMP5               ; if at zero, bail out.  restore Y
000845r 3  38               sec                     ; and return.
000846r 3  4C rr rr         jmp  CTRLEND            ; C=1 means eat keystroke w/o save to buffer or echo
000849r 3               DOCV_SK1:
000849r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
00084Br 3  18               clc
00084Cr 3  69 03            adc #3                 ; skip over address and length now
00084Er 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000850r 3  90 02            bcc  DOCV_SK2
000852r 3  E6 F3            inc TEMP2+1
000854r 3               DOCV_SK2:
000854r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000856r 3               DOCV_LP0:
000856r 3  C4 D4            cpy TIBEND
000858r 3  F0 0C            beq  DOCV_END
00085Ar 3  B1 F2            lda (TEMP2),y
00085Cr 3  F0 08            beq DOCV_END
00085Er 3  91 F0            sta (TEMP1),y
000860r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR      ; remember to write to the screen!
000863r 3  C8               iny
000864r 3  80 F0            bra DOCV_LP0
000866r 3               DOCV_END:
000866r 3  A9 20            lda #$20
000868r 3  91 F0            sta (TEMP1),y        ; tokenizer won't work w/o an extra space?
00086Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR       ;
00086Dr 3  C8               iny
00086Er 3  91 F0            sta (TEMP1),y        ; tokenizer won't work w/o an extra space?
000870r 3  C8               iny
000871r 3               
000871r 3  A9 00            lda #0              ; add a zero
000873r 3  91 F0            sta (TEMP1),y
000875r 3  88               dey                  ; new
000876r 3  98               tya
000877r 3  18               clc
000878r 3  65 FC            adc TEMP5
00087Ar 3  A8               tay
00087Br 3               
00087Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr DUMPREG         ; temp5 is at $FC, and also look at Y
00087Er 3               
00087Er 3  38               sec                  ; C=1 means eat keystroke w/o save to buffer or echo
00087Fr 3  4C rr rr         jmp CTRLEND
000882r 3               
000882r 3               
000882r 3               UPPER_END:
000882r 3               ;

000882r 3               ;.macro WERR errptr              -- write error using
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000882r 3               ;  definitions in hyforth.s
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ; DEBUG := 1        ; enable inclusion of debug code
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ; INROM = $A000
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ; malloc stack and managed memory area
000882r 3               ; both move downwards like other stacks
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;MEMEND = $01FE            ; pointers DOWN from MEMEND+MEMSTK (store-and-decrement)
000882r 3               ;MEMTOP = $8000            ; malloc allocates DOWN from MEMTOP
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;INBUF = $0400             ; see stacks segment
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000882r 3               ;  HyFedit definitions
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               HFE_EOL = $00              ; end of line
000882r 3               HFE_EOR = $09              ; tab means end of row
000882r 3               HFE_HZC = $2D              ; for horizontal frame '-'
000882r 3               HFE_VTC = $7C              ; for vertical frame   '|'
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;.segment "ZEROPAGE"
000882r 3               ; **** hyforth.s ZP
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;.org $C0
000882r 3               ;HFE_BPTR:   .res 2             ; pointer into 1K buffer      $C0
000882r 3               ;HFE_CH:     .res 1             ; cursor horizontal           $C2
000882r 3               ;HFE_CV:     .res 1             ; cursor vertical             $C3
000882r 3               ;HFE_T0:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C4
000882r 3               ;HFE_T0H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C5
000882r 3               ;HFE_T1:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C6
000882r 3               ;HFE_T1H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C7
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;TEMP0:     .res 2             ;  DUMPREG                    $C8
000882r 3               ;TEMP8:     .res 2             ;  Hstring macro              $CA
000882r 3               ;TEMP9:     .res 2             ;  Imm                        $CC
000882r 3               ;MEMPTR:    .res 2             ;  malloc                     $CE
000882r 3               ;MEMLAST:   .res 2             ;  malloc                     $D0
000882r 3               ;TIB:       .res 2             ; pointer to input buffer     $D2
000882r 3               ;TIBEND:    .res 2             ; pointer to end of TIB       $D4
000882r 3               ;DFLAG:     .res 1             ; debug flag                  $D6
000882r 3               ;ERRFLAG:   .res 1             ; error type, 0 = none        $D7
000882r 3               ;ERRPTR:    .res 2             ; ptr to mitigation/message   $D8
000882r 3               ;TEMP1:     .res 2     ; first, COPYTORAM, MEMCPY            $F0
000882r 3               ;TEMP2:     .res 2     ; second, COPYTORAM, MEMCPY           $F2
000882r 3               ;TEMP3:     .res 2     ; third, COPYTORAM, MEMCPY            $F4
000882r 3               ;TEMP4:     .res 2     ; fourth,                             $F6
000882r 3               ;NXTTOK:    .res 2     ; next token in tib (INBUF)           $F8
000882r 3               ;TEMP5:     .res 1     ;    WORDS, WFIND, MEMCPY             $FC
000882r 3               ;TEMP6:     .res 1     ;    WORDS, DIGCONT, TEXTGET          $FD
000882r 3               ;TEMP7:     .res 2     ;    AUTOLOAD                         $FE
000882r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;     hyforth.s buffers
000882r 3               ;.segment "BUFFERS"
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;   *** at $0400
000882r 3               ;INBUF:
000882r 3               ;      .res 256
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;   *** at $0600
000882r 3               ;MEMSTK:                      ; memory manager
000882r 3               ;      .res 510
000882r 3               ;      .res 2
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000882r 3               ;  hyforth.s starts at $0800
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;  space reserved for *stuff* at $0806 to $0825
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;HFE_RSV:           ; at $0806
000882r 3               ;HFECOLS:        ; offsets to data in RAM
000882r 3               ;    .byte $3F    ; 63 columns w/ $00 or $09 in rightmost
000882r 3               ;HFEROWS:
000882r 3               ;    .byte $10    ; 16 rows
000882r 3               ;HFEBUF:
000882r 3               ;    .res 2       ; addr to buffer
000882r 3               ;             ideally, we would allocate 1K with 'malloc', then copy
000882r 3               ;             the address to HFEBUF in HyForth, then call HFE_COLD
000882r 3               ;             ...or if buffer already got stuff, call HFE_WARM
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;   .res 6
000882r 3               ;HFE_TITLE:
000882r 3               ;    .byte "Generic ScreenX"
000882r 3               ;    .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0    ; title of screen - zero terminated string, up to 12 chars incl \0
000882r 3               ;
000882r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000882r 3               ;.segment "FORTH_EDIT"
000882r 3               ;                             ; this will be at $F500 until further notice...
000882r 3               HFE_WARM:
000882r 3  A9 01          lda #1                      ; set 'warm start' flag, HFE_T0H
000884r 3  85 C5          sta HFE_T0H
000886r 3  80 06          bra hfe_warmover                    ; skip over next inst
000888r 3               HFE_COLD:
000888r 3  64 C5          stz HFE_T0H
00088Ar 3  64 C2          stz HFE_CH                  ; set cursor position
00088Cr 3  64 C3          stz HFE_CV
00088Er 3               hfe_warmover:
00088Er 3  AD rr rr       lda HFEBUF+1
000891r 3  85 C1          sta HFE_BPTR+1
000893r 3  AD rr rr       lda HFEBUF                   ; find out where screen buffer is
000896r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
000898r 3  20 rr rr       jsr HFE_SCRINIT
00089Br 3               
00089Br 3  4C rr rr       jmp HFE_START
00089Er 3               
00089Er 3               HFE_START:
00089Er 3  20 rr rr       jsr  HFE_SCRINIT
0008A1r 3  20 rr rr       jsr  HFE_REDRAW
0008A4r 3               
0008A4r 3               HFE_PARSE:
0008A4r 3               
0008A4r 3               hfe_getline:
0008A4r 3               
0008A4r 3               HFE_EXIT:
0008A4r 3  60             rts
0008A5r 3               ;
0008A5r 3               ; end of main HyFedit code
0008A5r 3               ;
0008A5r 3               ;-------------------------------------------
0008A5r 3               ;   Screen Init
0008A5r 3               HFE_SCRINIT:
0008A5r 3                 ;jsr ANSICLS
0008A5r 3  A9 40          lda #$40         ; 64 columns, including line ending
0008A7r 3  8D rr rr       sta HFECOLS
0008AAr 3  A9 10          lda #$10         ; 16 rows
0008ACr 3  8D rr rr       sta HFEROWS
0008AFr 3  60             rts
0008B0r 3               
0008B0r 3               ;-------------------------------------------
0008B0r 3               ;   Redraw
0008B0r 3               HFE_REDRAW:
0008B0r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_dline                     ; draw top line
0008B3r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_title                     ; draw title line
0008B6r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_dline                     ; draw border
0008B9r 3  AE rr rr       ldx HFEROWS
0008BCr 3  A9 30          lda #$30
0008BEr 3  85 C6          sta HFE_T1                              ; for line #
0008C0r 3               HFE_LP0:
0008C0r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_bline
0008C3r 3               ;  iny
0008C3r 3               ;  tya
0008C3r 3  AD rr rr       lda HFECOLS
0008C6r 3  18             clc
0008C7r 3  65 C0          adc HFE_BPTR                           ; update pointer to get to
0008C9r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
0008CBr 3  90 02          bcc HFE_SK0                            ; next line.
0008CDr 3  E6 C1          inc HFE_BPTR + 1
0008CFr 3               HFE_SK0:
0008CFr 3  A5 C6          lda HFE_T1                             ; for printed line count
0008D1r 3  1A             inc a                                  ; increment for next
0008D2r 3  C9 3A          cmp #$3A                               ; line, and see if need
0008D4r 3  D0 03          bne HFE_SK1                            ; alpha hex yet.
0008D6r 3  18             clc
0008D7r 3  69 07          adc #7                                 ; yup.  It's now 'A'
0008D9r 3               HFE_SK1:
0008D9r 3  85 C6          sta HFE_T1
0008DBr 3  CA             dex
0008DCr 3  D0 E2          bne HFE_LP0
0008DEr 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_dline                     ; draw bottom line
0008E1r 3  60             rts
0008E2r 3               ;
0008E2r 3               ;  *** draw horizontal line
0008E2r 3               ;
0008E2r 3               hfe_draw_dline:
0008E2r 3  AD rr rr       lda HFECOLS
0008E5r 3  18             clc
0008E6r 3  69 04          adc #4
0008E8r 3  AA             tax
0008E9r 3               hfe_ddloop:
0008E9r 3  A9 2D          lda #'-'
0008EBr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0008EEr 3  CA             dex
0008EFr 3  D0 F8          bne hfe_ddloop
0008F1r 3  A9 0D 20 rr    WCRLF_np                      ; CRLF w/o push/pull of A
0008F5r 3  rr A9 0A 20  
0008F9r 3  rr rr        
0008FBr 3  60             rts
0008FCr 3               ;
0008FCr 3               ;  *** draw title line
0008FCr 3               ;
0008FCr 3               hfe_draw_title:
0008FCr 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
0008FEr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000901r 3  A0 14          ldy #20
000903r 3  AE rr rr       ldx HFECOLS
000906r 3  E8             inx
000907r 3  A9 20          lda #$20
000909r 3               hfe_dtloop0:
000909r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
00090Cr 3  CA             dex
00090Dr 3  88             dey
00090Er 3  D0 F9          bne hfe_dtloop0
000910r 3                 ;
000910r 3  A9 31          lda #$31              ; set color to red
000912r 3  85 BA          sta Z_ANSI1
000914r 3  A9 33          lda #$33
000916r 3  85 BB          sta Z_ANSI1+1
000918r 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL         ; esc[31m
00091Br 3                 ;
00091Br 3  A0 00          ldy #0
00091Dr 3  A9 rr          lda #>HFE_TITLE
00091Fr 3  85 C5          sta HFE_T0 + 1
000921r 3  A9 rr          lda #<HFE_TITLE
000923r 3  85 C4          sta HFE_T0
000925r 3               hfe_dtloop1:
000925r 3  B1 C4          lda (HFE_T0), y
000927r 3  F0 07          beq hfe_dtcon0
000929r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
00092Cr 3  C8             iny
00092Dr 3  CA             dex
00092Er 3  80 F5          bra hfe_dtloop1
000930r 3               hfe_dtcon0:
000930r 3  64 BA          stz Z_ANSI1         ; set color back to normal
000932r 3  64 BB          stz Z_ANSI1+1
000934r 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL         ; esc[0m
000937r 3  A9 20          lda #$20
000939r 3               hfe_dtloop2:
000939r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
00093Cr 3  CA             dex
00093Dr 3  D0 FA          bne hfe_dtloop2
00093Fr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000942r 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
000944r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000947r 3  A9 0D 20 rr    WCRLF_np                      ; CRLF w/o push/pull of A
00094Br 3  rr A9 0A 20  
00094Fr 3  rr rr        
000951r 3  60             rts
000952r 3               ;
000952r 3               ;  *** draw line from buffer
000952r 3               ;
000952r 3               hfe_draw_bline:
000952r 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
000954r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000957r 3                 ;
000957r 3  A9 32          lda #$32              ; set color to green
000959r 3  85 BA          sta Z_ANSI1
00095Br 3  A9 33          lda #$33
00095Dr 3  85 BB          sta Z_ANSI1+1
00095Fr 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL
000962r 3                 ;
000962r 3  A5 C6          lda HFE_T1                     ; line #
000964r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR                 ; print it
000967r 3                 ;
000967r 3  64 BA          stz Z_ANSI1
000969r 3  64 BB          stz Z_ANSI1+1        ; and back to normal
00096Br 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL
00096Er 3                 ;
00096Er 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
000970r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000973r 3  A0 00          ldy #0
000975r 3               hfe_db_loop0:
000975r 3  B1 C0          lda (HFE_BPTR),y                ; if \0 or tab,
000977r 3  F0 1A          beq hfe_db_done                ; line is done
000979r 3  C9 09          cmp #$09                       ; and we can skip
00097Br 3  F0 16          beq hfe_db_done                ; to next line.
00097Dr 3  C9 1F          cmp #$1F
00097Fr 3  90 0A          bcc hfe_db_spcsub              ; don't print non-printables
000981r 3  C9 7F          cmp #$7F                       ;..there SHOULDN'T be any
000983r 3  B0 06          bcs hfe_db_spcsub              ; but who knows....
000985r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR                 ; padding out line.
000988r 3  C8             iny
000989r 3  80 EA          bra hfe_db_loop0
00098Br 3               hfe_db_spcsub:
00098Br 3  A9 20          lda #$20
00098Dr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000990r 3  C8             iny
000991r 3  80 E2          bra hfe_db_loop0               ; substitute spc for non-printables
000993r 3               hfe_db_done:
000993r 3  84 C7          sty HFE_T1H                    ; swap around to calc extra spaces
000995r 3  AD rr rr       lda HFECOLS
000998r 3  38             sec
000999r 3  E5 C7          sbc HFE_T1H
00099Br 3  A8             tay
00099Cr 3  A9 20          lda #$20
00099Er 3               hfe_db_loop1:
00099Er 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR                ; pad out rest of line
0009A1r 3  88             dey
0009A2r 3  D0 FA          bne  hfe_db_loop1
0009A4r 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
0009A6r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0009A9r 3  A9 0D 20 rr    WCRLF_np                      ; CRLF w/o push/pull of A
0009ADr 3  rr A9 0A 20  
0009B1r 3  rr rr        
0009B3r 3               
0009B3r 3               ;  ldy HFE_T1H               ; restore Y and return to calc offset to next line...
0009B3r 3               ;  jsr DUMPREG
0009B3r 3  60             rts
0009B4r 3               
0009B4r 3               ;
0009B4r 3               ;                 update a line on screen
0009B4r 3               ;
0009B4r 3               HFE_UPDATE:
0009B4r 3  A0 00          ldy #0
0009B6r 3  A2 C3          ldx #HFE_CV
0009B8r 3               hfe_u_loop0:         ; count zeros/tabs up to current cursor pos
0009B8r 3  B1 C0          lda (HFE_BPTR),y
0009BAr 3  F0 07          beq  HFEU_ZCNT
0009BCr 3  C9 09          cmp #$09
0009BEr 3  F0 03          beq  HFEU_ZCNT
0009C0r 3  C8             iny
0009C1r 3  80 F5          bra hfe_u_loop0
0009C3r 3               HFEU_ZCNT:
0009C3r 3  C8             iny
0009C4r 3  98             tya
0009C5r 3  18             clc
0009C6r 3  65 C0          adc HFE_BPTR         ; update HFE_BPTR
0009C8r 3  90 02          bcc HFEU_SKIP
0009CAr 3  E6 C1          inc HFE_BPTR + 1
0009CCr 3               HFEU_SKIP:
0009CCr 3  A0 00          ldy #0
0009CEr 3  8A             txa
0009CFr 3  F0 03          beq HFEU_LFOUND
0009D1r 3  CA             dex
0009D2r 3  80 E4          bra hfe_u_loop0
0009D4r 3               HFEU_LFOUND:
0009D4r 3  A5 C2          lda HFE_CH
0009D6r 3  85 C6          sta HFE_T1           ; backup horizontal cursor pos
0009D8r 3  A9 00          lda #0
0009DAr 3  85 C2          sta HFE_CH          ; cuz we wanna draw from beginning of line
0009DCr 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_place_cur   ; place the cursor
0009DFr 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_bline  ; and draw from the current HFE_PTR until line end
0009E2r 3  A5 FE          lda HFE_T2
0009E4r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
0009E6r 3  A5 FF          lda HFE_T2 + 1       ; restore HFE_PTR and HFE_CH
0009E8r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
0009EAr 3  A5 C6          lda HFE_T1
0009ECr 3  85 C2          sta HFE_CH
0009EEr 3  60             rts
0009EFr 3               
0009EFr 3               hfe_place_cur:
0009EFr 3  A5 C2          lda HFE_CH
0009F1r 3  18             clc
0009F2r 3  69 02          adc #2
0009F4r 3  85 F4          sta TEMP3
0009F6r 3  A5 C3          lda HFE_CV
0009F8r 3  18             clc
0009F9r 3  69 04          adc #4
0009FBr 3  85 F6          sta TEMP4
0009FDr 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSISCR
000A00r 3  60             rts
000A01r 3               ;
000A01r 3               ;   Forth heap subroutines
000A01r 3               ;
000A01r 3               FECPY:                    ; copy a z-termed string from an addr in TEMP1
000A01r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000A03r 3  80 0B            bra FECPYLP
000A05r 3               FECPY_TOC:
000A05r 3  A4 C2            ldy HFE_CH
000A07r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000A09r 3  38               sec
000A0Ar 3  E5 C2            sbc HFE_CH
000A0Cr 3  B0 02            bcs  FECPYLP
000A0Er 3  C6 F1            dec TEMP1+1
000A10r 3               FECPYLP:                         ; assumes zero term'd string
000A10r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000A12r 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y
000A14r 3  F0 0B            beq FEADDEND
000A16r 3  98               tya
000A17r 3  1A               inc
000A18r 3  1A               inc
000A19r 3  CD rr rr         cmp HFECOLS                  ; max of 63 chars, rest trunc'd off
000A1Cr 3  F0 03            beq FEADDEND                 ; this will be fixed for something more flexible
000A1Er 3  C8               iny
000A1Fr 3  80 EF            bra FECPYLP
000A21r 3               FEADDEND:
000A21r 3  C8               iny
000A22r 3  A9 00            lda #0
000A24r 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y                ; write zero at end of line
000A26r 3  88               dey
000A27r 3  84 F0            sty TEMP1
000A29r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
000A2Br 3  60               rts
000A2Cr 3               ;
000A2Cr 3               ;
000A2Cr 3               FELIN:           ; CALC ROW ADDRESS IN BUFFER, from TEMP2, ends up in TEMP2
000A2Cr 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
000A2Er 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
000A30r 3  29 0F            and #$0F                     ; max rows are 16, mask off anything else
000A32r 3  18               clc
000A33r 3  2A               rol
000A34r 3  2A               rol
000A35r 3  2A               rol                          ; multiply times 64
000A36r 3  2A               rol
000A37r 3  90 02            bcc  M40SK0
000A39r 3  26 F3            rol TEMP2+1
000A3Br 3               M40SK0:
000A3Br 3  2A               rol
000A3Cr 3  90 02            bcc  M40SK1
000A3Er 3  26 F3            rol TEMP2+1
000A40r 3               M40SK1:
000A40r 3  2A               rol
000A41r 3  90 02            bcc  M40SK2
000A43r 3  26 F3            rol  TEMP2+1
000A45r 3               M40SK2:
000A45r 3  18               clc
000A46r 3  6D rr rr         adc HFEBUF
000A49r 3  90 02            bcc  FEADDSK3
000A4Br 3  E6 F3            inc TEMP2+1
000A4Dr 3               FEADDSK3:
000A4Dr 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000A4Fr 3  AD rr rr         lda HFEBUF+1
000A52r 3  18               clc
000A53r 3  65 F3            adc TEMP2+1                  ; dest address in TEMP2
000A55r 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1
000A57r 3  60               rts
000A58r 3               
000A58r 3               ;
000A58r 3               HFE_END:

000A58r 2               .segment "FORTH_PAGED_ROM"
000000r 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;    hyf_rom.s -- COPYTORAM
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               .include "hyf_rom.s"
000000r 3               ;
000000r 3               ;  COPYTORAM  - This HAS to sit at $A000 or the HyForth engine in OSROM will NOT find it.
000000r 3               ;    so....this always needs to be the first thing in the library image before the heap,
000000r 3               ;    therefore .org $A000, .include 'hyf_rom.s', .org $0800, ...(heap).
000000r 3               ;
000000r 3               COPYMAIN = COPYSTART
000000r 3               RAMST = RAMSTART
000000r 3               COPYENDS = ends - RAMSTART + COPYSTART
000000r 3               
000000r 3               COPYTORAM:
000000r 3  4C rr rr         jmp CTRSTART                     ; copies from mainoff thru endsoff to ramstart
000003r 3  rr               .byte >h_exit
000004r 3  rr               .byte <h_exit
000005r 3  rr               .byte >ends
000006r 3               CTRSTART:
000006r 3               ;
000006r 3               ;    the 10-23-26 1920 version
000006r 3               ;
000006r 3               ;    reworked to used ZP_TEMP_VEC, ZP_TEMP_VEC2, ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000006r 3               ;                     mainoff      endsoff       ramstart
000006r 3               ;
000006r 3  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
00000Ar 3  rr A9 0A 20  
00000Er 3  rr rr        
000010r 3               ;    lda #1
000010r 3               ;    sta supprint               ; supress printing of progress
000010r 3  A9 A1            lda #>COPYMAIN
000012r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC+1
000014r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000017r 3  A9 00            lda #<COPYMAIN
000019r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC
00001Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
00001Er 3  A9 2B            lda #'+'
000020r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000023r 3  A9 79            lda #<COPYENDS
000025r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC2
000027r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
00002Ar 3  A9 BA            lda #>COPYENDS
00002Cr 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC2+1
00002Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000031r 3                   ;
000031r 3  A9 rr            lda #>RAMST
000033r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC3+1
000035r 3  A9 rr            lda #<RAMST
000037r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000039r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MEMCPY
00003Cr 3  A9 20            lda #$20
00003Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000041r 3  A9 66            lda #'f'

00005Br 2               ;

00005Br 2               COPYSTART := $A100              ; marks beginning of copy in ROM space
00005Br 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------

000000r 2               RAMSTART:

000003r 2  4C rr rr         jmp reset

000006r 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------

000006r 2               HFE_RSV:

00002Ar 2  69 62 20 61

000035r 2  00              .byte 0

000036r 2  B2 BB 00 00      LIBENTRY (LT_FCORE + COPYSTART - RAMSTART), 0
00003Ar 2  67 BE 00 00      LIBENTRY (LT_FEXTRA + COPYSTART - RAMSTART), 0
00003Er 2  ED C0 00 00      LIBENTRY (LT_ANSI + COPYSTART - RAMSTART), 0

00005Ar 2  xx xx xx xx  
00005Er 2  xx xx xx xx  
000076r 2               lib_jumps_end:
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;    error/system msg jump table  - see upper.s for ERR messages
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               LASTERR = 8
000076r 2               err_jumptable:
000076r 2  xx xx            .res 2                    ; padding for ???
000078r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry RPTR_ERR              ; RT stack full/empty  - error $01
00007Ar 2  rr rr            ERR_entry SPTR_ERR              ; DS stack full/empty  - error $02
00007Cr 2  rr rr            ERR_entry DIV_ERR               ; divide by zero - error $03
00007Er 2  rr rr            ERR_entry OOM_ERR               ; out of memory  - error $04
000080r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry UKW_ERR               ; no existing word - error $05
000082r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry SEC_ERR               ; writing to dangerous RAM areas - error $06
000084r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry SYS_ERR               ; error on return from SYSCALL - error $07
000086r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry PAD_ERR               ; PAD out of memory - error $08
000088r 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0   ; extra padding, dynamically add more messages
00008Cr 2  00 00        
00008Er 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
000092r 2  00 00        
000094r 2  00 00            .byte 0, 0                     ; keep to 16 entries
000096r 2               err_jumps_end:
000096r 2               ;
000096r 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
000096r 2               ;
000096r 2               ;   'RAMCODE' - subroutines that need to be in RAM, or at least part
000096r 2               ;  of them.
000096r 2               ;
000096r 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
000096r 2               ;
000096r 2               ;    FLAGSET  - 65C02 only; uses RMBx/SMBx
000096r 2               ;     can just use RMBx / SMBx FLAGS if you know which bit...
000096r 2               ;    but if you need to decide at runtime you need this.
000096r 2               ;
000096r 2               RSBOP:
000096r 2  00               .byte 0
000097r 2  A3               .byte FLAGS
000098r 2  60               rts
000099r 2               FLAGSET:               ; bit # to chg is in X, neg bit means set
000099r 2  8A               txa
00009Ar 2  29 07            and #$07           ; clear all bits but functional ones
00009Cr 2  0A               asl
00009Dr 2  0A               asl
00009Er 2  0A               asl
00009Fr 2  0A               asl
0000A0r 2  09 07            ora #$07
0000A2r 2  E0 80            cpx #$80
0000A4r 2  30 02            bmi FGFINISH       ; will be RMBx
0000A6r 2  09 80            ora #$80           ; will be SMBx
0000A8r 2               FGFINISH:
0000A8r 2  8D rr rr         sta RSBOP
0000ABr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
0000AEr 2  4C rr rr         jmp RSBOP
0000B1r 2               ;
0000B1r 2               ;--------------------------------- CLEAR ------------------------------
0000B1r 2               ;
0000B1r 2               ;  zero out $C8 - $FF, meet and greet
0000B1r 2               ;  zero out INBUF
0000B1r 2               ;  and clear SCRBUF
0000B1r 2               ;
0000B1r 2               CLEAR:
0000B1r 2  A9 FF            lda #$FF
0000B3r 2  85 D6            sta DFLAG                       ; debug OFF by default
0000B5r 2               zpclear:
0000B5r 2  A2 A0            ldx #ZPSTART                    ; whoops, this is all the ZP that is used
0000B7r 2  A9 00            lda #0
0000B9r 2               zerolp:
0000B9r 2  95 00            sta  $00,x
0000BBr 2  E8               inx
0000BCr 2  D0 FB            bne zerolp
0000BEr 2  A2 00            ldx  #0
0000C0r 2               inbufzlp:                           ; and then clear out INBUF
0000C0r 2  9D rr rr         sta  INBUF,x
0000C3r 2  E8               inx
0000C4r 2  D0 FA            bne  inbufzlp
0000C6r 2  A2 00            ldx  #0
0000C8r 2               stackslp:                           ; and then clear out DS / RT
0000C8r 2  9D rr rr         sta  DS,x
0000CBr 2  E8               inx
0000CCr 2  D0 FA            bne  stackslp
0000CEr 2                       ;
0000CEr 2  20 rr rr         jsr CLR_SCREENBUF               ; it does this
0000D1r 2                       ;  meet and greet
0000D1r 2  A0 00            ldy  #0
0000D3r 2               hywelclp:
0000D3r 2  B9 rr rr         lda  HYWELCOME,y
0000D6r 2  F0 06            beq  CLREXIT
0000D8r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0000DBr 2  C8               iny
0000DCr 2  80 F5            bra  hywelclp
0000DEr 2               CLREXIT:
0000DEr 2  20 rr rr         jsr print_libtable
0000E1r 2  60               rts
0000E2r 2               
0000E2r 2               CLR_SCREENBUF:
0000E2r 2               ;               clear screen buffer; default is 16 rows of 63 + 1 columns
0000E2r 2               ;           assembly format is two columns with 16 rows of 31 + 1 columns each
0000E2r 2               ;
0000E2r 2  AD rr rr         lda HFEROWS
0000E5r 2  85 FC            sta TEMP5                       ; count rows from HFEROWS
0000E7r 2  A9 00            lda #<SCRBUF
0000E9r 2  85 C8            sta TEMP0
0000EBr 2  A9 7C            lda #>SCRBUF
0000EDr 2  85 C9            sta TEMP0 + 1
0000EFr 2               clrscrbuf0:
0000EFr 2  A9 20            lda #$20
0000F1r 2  AE rr rr         ldx HFECOLS                       ; count down from HFECOLS
0000F4r 2  CA               dex
0000F5r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0000F7r 2               clrscrbuf1:
0000F7r 2  91 C8            sta (TEMP0),y
0000F9r 2  C8               iny
0000FAr 2  CA               dex
0000FBr 2  D0 FA            bne clrscrbuf1               ; and last col in row is zero, so...
0000FDr 2  A9 00            lda #0                       ; all rows zero term'd but
0000FFr 2  91 C8            sta (TEMP0),y                ; don't know if really need
000101r 2  A5 C8            lda TEMP0                    ; to clear rows this way or not, yet.
000103r 2  18               clc
000104r 2  6D rr rr         adc HFECOLS
000107r 2  90 02            bcc clrscrbuf2
000109r 2  E6 C9            inc TEMP0+1
00010Br 2               clrscrbuf2:
00010Br 2  85 C8            sta TEMP0
00010Dr 2  C6 FC            dec TEMP5
00010Fr 2  D0 DE            bne clrscrbuf0
000111r 2  60               rts

000112r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000112r 2               ;        core and primitives - original AGSB hardcoded dictionary
000112r 2               ;
000112r 2               ;        loading order, what's where:
000112r 2               ;
000112r 2               ; HEAP
000112r 2               ;    core.s
000112r 2               ;  -----------------------------------------------------------
000112r 2               ;  @ ! lit [ ] , : :$ ; ;$ I .R .S xR xS ?R ?S
000112r 2               ;  dup swap rot over drop 0 to 9 $0 to $F %0 %1 < > <= >= = <>
000112r 2               ;  0= bool FALSE TRUE not neg and or nand xor
000112r 2               ;  + - key emit ord . .be .C spc cr c! c@ words w&
000112r 2               ;  in> >in rp sp x@ pp mp r> r> back here last
000112r 2               ;  bye exec reset abort debug
000112r 2               ;
000112r 2               ;    primitives.s - memory, printing, math
000112r 2               ;  ------------------------------------------------------------
000112r 2               ;  var cons malloc mlen mem@ pad@ pad! xpad
000112r 2               ;  hx2d dump .sz .rsz .psz .sn
000112r 2               ;  * / */ max min << >> cbit sbit tbit
000112r 2               ;
000112r 2               ;    hywords.s - ADDITIONS, HyFedit words, ANSI, memory extras
000112r 2               ;  -------------------------------------------------------------
000112r 2               ;  bload cload autoload lib libs free
000112r 2               ;  syscall disasm memcpy
000112r 2               ;  memw@ memwn@ wmemn! cmemn! rcmemn!
000112r 2               ;  rand rand32 rseed Acol Aclr Ascr
000112r 2               ;  ywrite ytest yinit
000112r 2               ;  ferc@ fecol! ferow! felin feadd feclr fedit febuf fedisp
000112r 2               ;  fa_mo@ fa_ms@
000112r 2               ;
000112r 2               ;  ---- and EXIT is always last ----
000112r 2               ;  exit
000112r 2               ;
000112r 2               ;
000112r 2               ; ROM and ENGINE
000112r 2               ;    upper.s
000112r 2               ;    hyf_edit.s
000112r 2               ;
