000112r 3               ;------------ CRITICAL CORE PRIMITIVES (AGSB and PGS) ----------------
000112r 3               ;   (the 'primitive' primitives)
000112r 3               ;  -----------------------------------------------------------
000112r 3               ;  @ ! lit [ ] , : :$ ; ;$ I .R .S xR xS ?R ?S
000112r 3               ;  dup swap rot over drop 0-9 $0-$F %0 %1 < > <= >= = <>
000112r 3               ;  0= bool FALSE TRUE not neg and or nand xor
000112r 3               ;  + - key emit ord . .be spc cr c! c@ words w&
000112r 3               ;  in> >in rp sp x@ pp mp r> r> back here last
000112r 3               ;  bye exec reset abort debug
000112r 3               ;  -----------------------------------------------------------
000112r 3               ;
000112r 3               ;   COMPILE (:), FINISH (;), FETCH (@), STORE (!)
000112r 3               ;      including:  keeps, this, next, finish
000112r 3               ;       ...and other stuff.
000112r 3               ;
000112r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000112r 3               ;
000112r 3               ; ( -- ) ae exit forth
000112r 3  00 00 03 62  def_word "bye", "bye", 0        ; use the 'woz' word (JSR), '^' to get back, if you just need Wozmon for a sec.
000116r 3  79 65        
000118r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WWARMSTART_ADDR
00011Br 3  4C rr rr         jmp MON_START               ; jump to WOZMON, return with warm start
00011Er 3               WWARMSTART_ADDR:
00011Er 3  A9 46            lda #'F'
000120r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000123r 3  A9 57            lda #'W'
000125r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000128r 3  A9 2D            lda #'-'
00012Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00012Dr 3  A9 rr            lda #>F_warm
00012Fr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000132r 3  A9 rr            lda #<F_warm
000134r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000137r 3  60               rts
000138r 3               ;
000138r 3               ; ( -- ) ae abort
000138r 3  rr rr 05 61  def_word "abort", "abort_", 0
00013Cr 3  62 6F 72 74  
000140r 3  4C rr rr         jmp abort
000143r 3               ;
000143r 3               ; ( -- ) resets buffer to INBUF
000143r 3  rr rr 05 72  def_word "reset", "reset_", 0
000147r 3  65 73 65 74  
00014Br 3  4C rr rr         jmp reset
00014Er 3               ;
00014Er 3               ; ( a -- ) execute a jump to a reference at top of data stack
00014Er 3               ;
00014Er 3               ;   e.g.  if you have address of a CFA, and ': doit exec ;$ ;'
00014Er 3               ;   then 'doit' will execute the word and return safely
00014Er 3               ;
00014Er 3               ;
00014Er 3  rr rr 04 65  def_word "exec", "exec", 0
000152r 3  78 65 63     
000155r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
000158r 3  6C F0 00         jmp (TEMP1)        ; assumes an address on top of DS
00015Br 3               
00015Br 3               ;
00015Br 3               ; ( a -- ) execute a jump to arbitrary code (which better return!)
00015Br 3  rr rr 04 78  def_word "xjsr", "xjsr", 0
00015Fr 3  6A 73 72     
000162r 3  4C rr rr         jmp JSRSKIP
000165r 3               DOJSR:
000165r 3  20 00 00         .byte $20, 0, 0
000168r 3  4C rr rr         jmp JSREND
00016Br 3               JSRSKIP:
00016Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
00016Er 3  A2 01            ldx #1
000170r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000172r 3  9D rr rr         sta DOJSR,x
000175r 3  E8               inx
000176r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000178r 3  9D rr rr         sta DOJSR,x
00017Br 3  4C rr rr         jmp DOJSR
00017Er 3               JSREND:
00017Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000181r 3               
000181r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000181r 3               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to a reference at IP
000181r 3  rr rr 02 3A  def_word ":$", "docode", 0
000185r 3  24           
000186r 3  6C EC 00         jmp (INSTPTR)                 ;  DOCODE (thus the ':')
000189r 3               
000189r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000189r 3               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to next
000189r 3  rr rr 02 3B  def_word ";$", "donext", 0        ;  'next' (thus the ';')
00018Dr 3  24           
00018Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000191r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000191r 3               ;         set and clear flags bits and delimiter
000191r 3               ;
000191r 3  rr rr 03 66  def_word "fb!", "fbitset", 0
000195r 3  62 21        
000197r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
00019Ar 3  A6 F0            ldx TEMP1
00019Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr FLAGSET          ; kills X, but uses NO TEMP vars
00019Fr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0001A2r 3               ;
0001A2r 3  rr rr 02 66  def_word "f@", "flagget", 0
0001A6r 3  40           
0001A7r 3  A5 A3            lda FLAGS
0001A9r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0001ABr 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0001ADr 3  4C rr rr         jmp this
0001B0r 3               ;
0001B0r 3  rr rr 02 3F  def_word "?f", "whatsf", 0
0001B4r 3  66           
0001B5r 3  64 F2            stz TEMP2
0001B7r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
0001B9r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0001BCr 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0001BEr 3  29 07            and #$07
0001C0r 3  AA               tax
0001C1r 3  E8               inx
0001C2r 3  A9 00            lda #0
0001C4r 3  38               sec
0001C5r 3               WHATSFLP:
0001C5r 3  2A               rol
0001C6r 3  CA               dex
0001C7r 3  D0 FC            bne WHATSFLP
0001C9r 3  25 A3            and FLAGS
0001CBr 3  F0 04            beq WHATSCLR
0001CDr 3               WHATSSET:
0001CDr 3  C6 F2            dec TEMP2
0001CFr 3  C6 F3            dec TEMP2+1
0001D1r 3               WHATSCLR:
0001D1r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_1
0001D4r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0001D7r 3               ;
0001D7r 3               ;
0001D7r 3  rr rr 04 64  def_word "del@", "delimget", 0
0001DBr 3  65 6C 40     
0001DEr 3  A9 A2            lda #DELIM
0001E0r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0001E2r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0001E4r 3  4C rr rr         jmp this
0001E7r 3               ;
0001E7r 3  rr rr 04 64  def_word "del!", "delimst", 0
0001EBr 3  65 6C 21     
0001EEr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0001F1r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0001F3r 3  85 A2            sta DELIM
0001F5r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0001F8r 3               ;
0001F8r 3  rr rr 02 66  def_word "f!", "flagsset", 0
0001FCr 3  21           
0001FDr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
000200r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000202r 3  85 A3            sta FLAGS
000204r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000207r 3               ;
000207r 3               ; ( w a -- ) ; [a] = w    (w is word, a is an address)
000207r 3  rr rr 01 21  def_word "!", "store", 0
00020Br 3               storew:
00020Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1             ; get address, store in TEMP2
00020Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get data, store in TEMP1
000211r 3  A2 F2            ldx #TEMP2              ;  [a]
000213r 3  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1              ;   w
000215r 3  20 rr rr         jsr copyinto            ; copy TEMP2 stuff to addr in TEMP1 (opposite of @)..
000218r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next                ;            ...(see below)
00021Br 3               ;
00021Br 3               ;--------------------------------------------FETCH--------------------
00021Br 3               ; ( a -- w ) ; w = [a]
00021Br 3  rr rr 01 40  def_word "@", "fetch", 0      ; replace addr of data on top of DS, with data pointed to
00021Fr 3               fetchw:
00021Fr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get addr from DS
000222r 3  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
000224r 3  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
000226r 3  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom            ; copies data from [TEMP1] => TEMP2
000229r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000229r 3               ;            NEXT entry point for many AGSB primitives, and
000229r 3               ;            almost all other hard-coded words.
000229r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000229r 3                                           ; in fall through, this copies TEMP2 (in y) to TEMP1,
000229r 3                                           ; overwriting the address that was pulled from the stack.
000229r 3                                           ;
000229r 3                                           ; in theory, one could load y with any TEMPx and
000229r 3                                           ; call jump to 'copys' to finish off to 'next'.
000229r 3                                           ; in PRACTICE, AGSB didn't reuse this much.
000229r 3                                           ;
000229r 3               copys:                      ; copy from cell at y (zp) to TEMP1
000229r 3  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
00022Cr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00022Er 3  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000231r 3                                           ; 'keeps' is a shortcut to the 'second half' of the
000231r 3                                           ; (sta TEMP1) routine to return results; to push another
000231r 3                                           ; variable on the stack, do it explicitly.
000231r 3                                           ;
000231r 3                                           ; 'this' assumes 'keeps' has already been done.
000231r 3                                           ;  Almost every hard-coded word uses keeps, this, or next
000231r 3                                           ;  to end a word; 'next' ends with 'exit' of course.
000231r 3                                           ;
000231r 3               keeps:                      ; saves bytes since have to get here anyway
000231r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000233r 3               this:                       ; same as above
000233r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0             ; then push back on stack
000236r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000239r 3               ;
000239r 3               ;-----------------------IMMEDIATE, '[', ']', ','-----------------------
000239r 3  rr rr 01 49  def_word "I", "Imm", 0
00023Dr 3               wimm:                        ; jmp here if proccessing a compiled
00023Dr 3  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP+1           ;   word that needs to run 'immediate'.
00023Fr 3  85 CD            sta TEMP9+1
000241r 3  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP             ; get addr of 'last' compiled word, copy to TEMP4, add 2
000243r 3  18               clc
000244r 3  69 02            adc #2                   ; ..to find where length byte is...
000246r 3  85 CC            sta TEMP9
000248r 3  90 02            bcc IMMSKIP
00024Ar 3  E6 CD            inc TEMP9+1
00024Cr 3               IMMSKIP:
00024Cr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00024Er 3  B1 CC            lda (TEMP9),y
000250r 3  09 80            ora #$80                 ; ...set bit 7 and store
000252r 3  91 CC            sta (TEMP9),y
000254r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000257r 3               ;
000257r 3  rr rr 81 5B  def_word "[", "leftbrack", FLAG_IMM       ; switch to 'interpret'
00025Br 3  64 E0            stz STATUS
00025Dr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000260r 3               ;
000260r 3  rr rr 01 5D  def_word "]", "rtbrack", 0               ; switch back to 'compile'
000264r 3  A9 01            lda #1
000266r 3  85 E0            sta STATUS
000268r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
00026Br 3               ;
00026Br 3               ;
00026Br 3                                                        ; NOTE: 'lit' or similar will have pushed a value
00026Br 3                                                        ; or address onto stack; 'comma' stores it inline in word
00026Br 3                                                        ;
00026Br 3  rr rr 01 2C  def_word ",", "xcomma", 0                ; pull data from top of stack, store at 'here' (NEXTHEAP)
00026Fr 3                                                        ; , increment 'here' to point to next cell.
00026Fr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0                          ;
000272r 3  A0 00            ldy #0                               ;
000274r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000276r 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y                     ; POP DS TO TEMP1, [here] = TEMP1
000278r 3  C8               iny
000279r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00027Br 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
00027Dr 3  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                        ; here += 2
00027Fr 3  A9 02            lda #2
000281r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
000284r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000287r 3               
000287r 3               ;--------------------------------------------SEMIS-----------------
000287r 3  rr rr 81 3B  def_word ";", "semis",  FLAG_IMM
00028Br 3  A5 FA            lda BACKHEAP
00028Dr 3  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP                ; bring back BACKHEAP to LASTHEAP
00028Fr 3  A5 FB            lda BACKHEAP + 1
000291r 3  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
000293r 3               
000293r 3  64 E0            stz STATUS                  ; set status to 'interpret' (presumably from 'compile')
000295r 3               
000295r 3               finish:                         ; compiled words must end with exit
000295r 3  A9 rr            lda #<exit
000297r 3  85 EE            sta WORKREG
000299r 3  A9 rr            lda #>exit
00029Br 3  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1
00029Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma                  ; change NEXTHEAP to point to addr of 'exit',
0002A0r 3                                               ; and make sure is last entry in code table for word...
0002A0r 3                                               ; as all good Forth compiled words should do.
0002A0r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0002A3r 3               ;
0002A3r 3               ;
0002A3r 3               ;------------------------------------------COMPILE------------------
0002A3r 3  rr rr 01 3A  def_word ":", "colon", 0
0002A7r 3  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
0002A9r 3  85 FA            sta BACKHEAP                ; backup NEXTHEAP to BACKHEAP
0002ABr 3  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP + 1
0002ADr 3  85 FB            sta BACKHEAP + 1
0002AFr 3               
0002AFr 3  A9 01            lda #1                      ; set status to 'compile'
0002B1r 3  85 E0            sta STATUS
0002B3r 3               
0002B3r 3               COMPHEADER:
0002B3r 3               ; copy LASTHEAP into (NEXTHEAP)
0002B3r 3  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
0002B5r 3  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')
0002B8r 3  20 rr rr         jsr token                    ; get first token, the name of new word
0002BBr 3  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; copy it to heap: length and name
0002BDr 3                                                ; code field comes with later proc
0002BDr 3               COMPLOOP:
0002BDr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
0002BFr 3  C9 20            cmp #$20
0002C1r 3  F0 0B            beq COMPEND
0002C3r 3  C0 00            cpy #0                       ; if this is the length field...
0002C5r 3  D0 02            bne COMCOPY
0002C7r 3  09 40            ora #$40                     ; set bit six for 'compiled' word
0002C9r 3               COMCOPY:
0002C9r 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP), y
0002CBr 3  C8               iny
0002CCr 3  D0 EF            bne COMPLOOP
0002CEr 3               COMPEND:
0002CEr 3  98               tya                          ; and update NEXTHEAP  :  'here' incremented by length
0002CFr 3  A2 E6            ldx #(NEXTHEAP)
0002D1r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx                   ; 'here' now at CFA
0002D4r 3               
0002D4r 3               ;~~~~~~~~ all done....
0002D4r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next                     ; and then see below; compiled word will continue until ';'
0002D7r 3               ;
0002D7r 3               ;  That's the basic basics, now for the rest of the core...

00105Cr 3  CA               dex                     ; need it 0 - LASTLIB-1 indexed

00105Fr 3                   ;                       ; bounds checking?

0011A3r 3               
0011A3r 3               BLAGAIN:
0011A3r 3  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
0011A5r 3  85 FA            sta BACKHEAP                ; backup NEXTHEAP to BACKHEAP
0011A7r 3  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP + 1
0011A9r 3  85 FB            sta BACKHEAP + 1
0011ABr 3               
0011ABr 3               BLHEAD:
0011ABr 3  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
0011ADr 3  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')
0011B0r 3  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; copy it to heap: length and name
0011B2r 3                                                ; code field comes with later proc
0011B2r 3                                                ;
0011B2r 3                                                ; NOTE:  This cannot pull more than 255 bytes including
0011B2r 3                                                ;  leading or trailing zeros!
0011B2r 3                                                ;
0011B2r 3               BLZEROSKIP:
0011B2r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1), y
0011B4r 3  D0 03            bne BLNSK1                  ; skip any LEADING zeros
0011B6r 3  C8               iny
0011B7r 3  80 F9            bra BLZEROSKIP
0011B9r 3               BLNSK1:
0011B9r 3  98               tya
0011BAr 3  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
0011BCr 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0011BFr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
0011C1r 3               BLNLOOP:
0011C1r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1), y
0011C3r 3  D0 0E            bne BLCOPY
0011C5r 3  C8               iny
0011C6r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1), y
0011C8r 3  F0 05            beq BLSKIP0
0011CAr 3  88               dey
0011CBr 3  A9 00            lda #0
0011CDr 3  80 04            bra BLCOPY
0011CFr 3               BLSKIP0:
0011CFr 3  88               dey
0011D0r 3  88               dey
0011D1r 3  80 05            bra BLEND
0011D3r 3               BLCOPY:
0011D3r 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP), y
0011D5r 3  C8               iny
0011D6r 3  D0 E9            bne BLNLOOP
0011D8r 3               BLEND:
0011D8r 3  C8               iny
0011D9r 3  98               tya                          ; and update NEXTHEAP  :  'here' incremented by length
0011DAr 3  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP
0011DCr 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0011DFr 3  C8               iny
0011E0r 3  C8               iny
0011E1r 3  98               tya

0011E7r 3               BLFINISH:

0011EBr 3  A5 FB            lda BACKHEAP + 1
0011EDr 3  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
0011EFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr BLTESTEND

0011F4r 3  4C rr rr         jmp BLAGAIN                  ; next word
0011F7r 3               ;    jsr spush_0                 ; push next/last address?
0011F7r 3               BLENDEND:

001571r 3               ;
001571r 3               ;       search for a byte in memory. Do not search more than $FF bytes
001571r 3               ;    return OFFSET.  If no luck, return next page to search.
001571r 3               ;
001571r 3               ;    NOTE:  I need alternate ways to terminate this search, built in.
001571r 3               ;
001571r 3  rr rr 04 62  def_word "bin&", "binamp", 0
001575r 3  69 6E 26     
001578r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0   ; address to start
00157Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1   ; byte to search for.  ignore MSB
00157Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr BINAMP
001581r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0
001584r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
001587r 3               
001587r 3  rr rr 05 62  def_word "bind&", "binampd", 0   ; search to delimeter at DELIM
00158Br 3  69 6E 64 26  
00158Fr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0   ; address to start
001592r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1   ; byte to search for.  ignore MSB
001595r 3  20 rr rr         jsr BINAMPD
001598r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0
00159Br 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
00159Er 3               
00159Er 3               BINAMP:
00159Er 3  A0 00            ldy #0
0015A0r 3               BINAMPLP:
0015A0r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
0015A2r 3  C5 F2            cmp TEMP2
0015A4r 3  F0 05            beq BINAMP_FOUND
0015A6r 3  C8               iny
0015A7r 3  F0 07            beq BINAMP_NONE
0015A9r 3  80 F5            bra BINAMPLP
0015ABr 3               BINAMP_FOUND:
0015ABr 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0015ADr 3  84 F0            sty TEMP1          ; this is the offset, NOT the found address
0015AFr 3  60               rts
0015B0r 3               BINAMP_NONE:
0015B0r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1        ; and this is one page ahead, if NOT found
0015B2r 3               BINAMP_END:
0015B2r 3  60               rts
0015B3r 3               
0015B3r 3               BINAMPD:               ; search until found or delimeter
0015B3r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
0015B5r 3               BINAMPDLP:
0015B5r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
0015B7r 3  C5 F2            cmp TEMP2
0015B9r 3  F0 F0            beq BINAMP_FOUND
0015BBr 3  C5 A2            cmp DELIM
0015BDr 3  F0 05            beq BINAMPD_NONE
0015BFr 3  C8               iny
0015C0r 3  F0 EE            beq BINAMP_NONE    ; return next page if no results so far
0015C2r 3  80 F1            bra BINAMPDLP
0015C4r 3               BINAMPD_NONE:
0015C4r 3  A9 FF            lda #$FF           ; not found before delimeter
0015C6r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0015C8r 3  C8               iny                ; offset + 1 of delimeter
0015C9r 3  84 F0            sty TEMP1
0015CBr 3  60               rts
0015CCr 3               ;
0015CCr 3               ;    hfa.s has HyForth assembler words now
0015CCr 3               ;

001680r 3  3A               dec

00168Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr FECPY                    ; src addr in TEMP1 returns # of chars copied in Y

001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ;  hfa.s  - split from 'hywords.s' 11/27/26
001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ; enable (=1) 'HFA' in hyforth.s to load
001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001715r 3               ;                     HFA - HyForth mini-assembler
001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ;  Three passes of assembler
001715r 3               ;   1)  convert a 'mnemonic' line of assembly e.g. 'LDAiy F0' to machine code
001715r 3               ;          q^LDAiy F0^
001715r 3               ;          {_mnsz@ pad@ @ 3 + 3 insz   ( -- off )
001715r 3               ;          {_mno@ bin&                 ( -- bop )
001715r 3               ;          pad@ @ 6 + {_asz&           ( -- bop am )
001715r 3               ;          {_bop&                      ( -- f len op )
001715r 3               ;          pad@ @ 8 + c@ x2bin          ( -- obmsb f len op )
001715r 3               ;          pad@ @ 9 + c@ x2bin          ( -- oblsb obmsb f len op )
001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ;   2) use '{org}' to set origin of new code
001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
001715r 3               ;
001715r 3               ;  opcode family table
001715r 3  rr rr 06 7B  def_word "{_ops@", "faopsget", 0
001719r 3  5F 6F 70 73  
00171Dr 3  40           
00171Er 3  A9 8E            lda #<FA_ops
001720r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
001722r 3  A9 C7            lda #>FA_ops
001724r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps                   ; 'keeps' includes sta TEMP1+1 / jsr spush_0 / jmp next
001727r 3               ;
001727r 3               ;  opcode address mode table
001727r 3  rr rr 06 7B  def_word "{_adm@", "faadmget", 0
00172Br 3  5F 61 64 6D  
00172Fr 3  40           
001730r 3  A9 CE            lda #<FA_addr
001732r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
001734r 3  A9 C7            lda #>FA_addr
001736r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps
001739r 3               ;
001739r 3               ;  mnemonic string addr from DISASM
001739r 3  rr rr 07 7B  def_word "{_mnsz@", "famnszget", 0
00173Dr 3  5F 6D 6E 73  
001741r 3  7A 40        
001743r 3  A9 rr            lda #<MNEMONIC_STR
001745r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
001747r 3  A9 rr            lda #>MNEMONIC_STR
001749r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps
00174Cr 3               ;
00174Cr 3               ;  mnemonic offset table addr from DISASM

00175Er 3               ;
00175Er 3               ;

00175Er 3               ;            how to handle the 'token' post-parser.
00175Er 3               ;
00175Er 3               ; ( mnsa -- off )
00175Er 3               ;def_word "{_m2st&", "fam2sampt", 0    ; returns offset in mn string (command line)
00175Er 3               ;
00175Er 3               ;     note: calling token kills x, y, a, TEMP1, TEMP2, TEMP4, TEMP5, TEMP6
00175Er 3               ;      (at least) Best not to use any TEMPs until after call, if possible
00175Er 3               ;

00175Er 3               ;    lda NXTTOK

00179Er 3               FA_ASZ2AM:           ; address string to address mode  - TEMP4 has address, will return mode
00179Er 3  A0 00            ldy #0

0017D1r 3  8A               txa
0017D2r 3  38               sec
0017D3r 3  E9 30            sbc #$30
0017D5r 3  30 27            bmi  FA_ASZERR        ; not anything we can use

0017D9r 3  10 09            bpl  FA_ASZNOTDIG     ; not a digit 0-7
0017DBr 3  1A               inc                   ; shift to upper nybble, add one
0017DCr 3  0A               asl

0017E4r 3               FA_ASZNOTDIG:

0017E6r 3               FA_ASZ1LP:

00183Ar 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00183Cr 3  85 FD            sta TEMP6
00183Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr   BINAMPD    ; base op will be in TEMP2, opaddr in TEMP1, offset if found TEMP1
001841r 3                                  ; if none found TEMP1 = $FF MSB + offset LSB to next table
001841r 3               ;jsr DUMPREG          ;  $20DC
001841r 3  A4 F0            ldy TEMP1        ; save offset
001843r 3  E6 F6            inc TEMP4        ; inc fam #
001845r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
001847r 3  C9 07            cmp #(FA_FAMCNT+1)  ; done with all of them?
001849r 3  F0 47            beq   FA_BNOFAM  ; none there, skip down to 'exceptions' in ad-mode list
00184Br 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00184Dr 3  30 05            bmi   FA_NOTFAM   ; if $FF, skip down and continue loop
00184Fr 3                                   ; else FOUND
00184Fr 3  80 19            bra   FA_FAMFOUND  ; skip down to finish
001851r 3               
001851r 3               FA_SEQLJMP:
001851r 3  4C rr rr         jmp FA_BSEQOP
001854r 3               
001854r 3               FA_NOTFAM:
001854r 3  A5 FD            lda TEMP6
001856r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
001858r 3  98               tya
001859r 3  18               clc
00185Ar 3  65 FC            adc TEMP5
00185Cr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00185Er 3  90 02            bcc  FA_BSK0
001860r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
001862r 3               FA_BSK0:
001862r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
001864r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y      ; get next base op
001866r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1       ; save fer later
001868r 3  80 CC            bra FA_BOPLP0
00186Ar 3               
00186Ar 3               FA_FAMFOUND:          ; now look up family base op in addr mode table
00186Ar 3  C6 F6            dec TEMP4
00186Cr 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
00186Er 3  C9 03            cmp #3            ; check fam#, is it more or less than 3?
001870r 3  10 1C            bpl FA_BSK1
001872r 3  F0 1A            beq FA_BSK1
001874r 3                                     ; family 0,1,2 just have 1 address mode
001874r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2         ; so we already have the correct op code
001876r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1       ; save it.
001878r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
00187Ar 3  F0 0C            beq   FA_BSKSINGLE   ; single byte instruction for fam 0
00187Cr 3  C9 01            cmp #1
00187Er 3  F0 04            beq   FA_BJSR       ; it's JSR, fam 1
001880r 3  A9 02            lda #2            ; else it's a branch, fam 2
001882r 3  80 06            bra   FA_BSK2
001884r 3               FA_BJSR:
001884r 3  A9 03            lda #3
001886r 3  80 02            bra   FA_BSK2
001888r 3               FA_BSKSINGLE:
001888r 3  A9 01            lda #1
00188Ar 3               FA_BSK2:
00188Ar 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2              ; store length
00188Cr 3  80 66            bra   FA_BSK2END
00188Er 3               
00188Er 3               FA_BSK1:                  ; now lookup address mode
00188Er 3  A2 00            ldx  #0               ; 'normal' families
001890r 3  80 0A            bra   FA_BAAMLKLP
001892r 3               FA_BNOFAM:
001892r 3  A2 2A            ldx #(FA_anofam-FA_addr)   ; exceptions
001894r 3  A9 0F            lda #$0F
001896r 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4          ; exceptions are family 'F'
001898r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
00189Ar 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1        ; and we are match base code, not family code
00189Cr 3               FA_BAAMLKLP:
00189Cr 3               ;jsr   DUMPREG                ; $213D
00189Cr 3  BD CE C7         lda FA_addr,x
00189Fr 3               ;jsr DUMPREG                  ; $2143
00189Fr 3  F0 6C            beq   FA_BOPERR
0018A1r 3  C5 F7            cmp TEMP4+1             ; match fam base, NOT mn base  (except for exceptions, bwahah)
0018A3r 3  F0 07            beq   FA_BNXT0           ; go here if found?
0018A5r 3  8A               txa
0018A6r 3  18               clc
0018A7r 3  69 0E            adc #FA_AMRLEN      ; FA_AMRLEN is row width
0018A9r 3  AA               tax
0018AAr 3  80 F0            bra   FA_BAAMLKLP
0018ACr 3               
0018ACr 3               FA_BNXT0:
0018ACr 3  E8               inx                  ; cuz they are offset 1-14 in row, not 0
0018ADr 3  8A               txa
0018AEr 3  18               clc
0018AFr 3  65 F4            adc TEMP3            ; add address mode code
0018B1r 3  AA               tax
0018B2r 3  BD CE C7         lda FA_addr,x        ; get offset code for op
0018B5r 3  C9 FF            cmp #$FF
0018B7r 3               ;jsr DUMPREG              ; $215F
0018B7r 3  F0 54            beq FA_BOPERR
0018B9r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1            ; store offset and continue
0018BBr 3  18               clc
0018BCr 3  65 F2            adc TEMP2
0018BEr 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1
0018C0r 3  80 1E            bra FA_BNXT1
0018C2r 3               
0018C2r 3               FA_BSEQOP:            ; for RMB/SMB/BBR/BBS
0018C2r 3  A9 F0            lda #$F0
0018C4r 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4         ; family # is $F0
0018C6r 3  18               clc
0018C7r 3  65 F4            adc TEMP3        ; address mode for these is $10-$80 -> $00 - $70
0018C9r 3  18               clc
0018CAr 3  65 F2            adc TEMP2        ; add base op ($07, $87, $0F, $8F)
0018CCr 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1      ; op code
0018CEr 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1      ; zero out fer later
0018D0r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
0018D2r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0018D4r 3  29 08            and #$08
0018D6r 3  F0 02            beq FA_BSK4      ; is this BBR/BBS?
0018D8r 3  E6 F6            inc TEMP4        ; yes. family is $F1
0018DAr 3               FA_BSK4:
0018DAr 3  A9 02            lda #2
0018DCr 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2         ; length for all is 2
0018DEr 3               ;jsr DUMPREG
0018DEr 3  80 14            bra   FA_BSK2END
0018E0r 3               FA_BNXT1:
0018E0r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0018E2r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
0018E4r 3                                 ; get # bytes of inst in TEMP2
0018E4r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3    ; address mode?
0018E6r 3  C9 08            cmp #8
0018E8r 3  10 06            bpl   FA_BNXT2     ; more than...
0018EAr 3  F0 04            beq   FA_BNXT2    ; or equal to 8?  3 bytes per inst
0018ECr 3  A9 02            lda #2
0018EEr 3  80 02            bra   FA_BNXT3
0018F0r 3               FA_BNXT2:
0018F0r 3  A9 03            lda #3
0018F2r 3               FA_BNXT3:
0018F2r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0018F4r 3               FA_BSK2END:
0018F4r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
0018F6r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0018F8r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0     ; push family#
0018FBr 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
0018FDr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0018FFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0     ; push opcode
001902r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_1     ; push length of inst
001905r 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
001907r 3  85 A2            sta DELIM      ; restore previous DELIM
001909r 3  38               sec           ; all is good
00190Ar 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
00190Dr 3               
00190Dr 3               FA_BOPERR:
00190Dr 3  18               clc
00190Er 3  A9 FF            lda #$FF
001910r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
001912r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
001914r 3  4C rr rr         jmp this
001917r 3               ;
001917r 3               ;
001917r 3               ; ( addr -- opa )
001917r 3               ;
001917r 3  rr rr 06 7B  def_word "{_opa&", "faopasscan"
00191Br 3  5F 6F 70 61  
00191Fr 3  26           
001920r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0     ; addr in TEMP1
001923r 3  64 FC            stz TEMP5       ; count digits
001925r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
001927r 3  A0 FF            ldy #$FF
001929r 3               FA_OPASSLP:
001929r 3  C8               iny
00192Ar 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
00192Cr 3  F0 3E            beq FA_OPASNOT         ; end of string?  nuthin
00192Er 3  C9 20            cmp #$20
001930r 3  D0 F7            bne FA_OPASSLP
001932r 3               FA_OPASLP:
001932r 3  C8               iny
001933r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
001935r 3  F0 35            beq FA_OPASNOT
001937r 3  C9 20            cmp #$20
001939r 3  F0 F7            beq FA_OPASLP
00193Br 3  38               sec
00193Cr 3  E9 30            sbc #$30
00193Er 3  30 2C            bmi FA_OPASNOT         ; error, not good number
001940r 3  C9 0A            cmp #$0A
001942r 3  30 09            bmi FA_OPASFOUND
001944r 3  38               sec
001945r 3  E9 07            sbc #7
001947r 3  C9 10            cmp #$10
001949r 3  30 02            bmi FA_OPASFOUND
00194Br 3  80 1F            bra FA_OPASNOT        ; error, not good char
00194Dr 3               FA_OPASFOUND:
00194Dr 3  48               pha
00194Er 3  85 FD            sta TEMP6
001950r 3  A5 FC            lda TEMP5
001952r 3  6A               ror
001953r 3  90 10            bcc FA_OPSKIPPUSH
001955r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
001957r 3  0A               asl
001958r 3  2A               rol
001959r 3  2A               rol
00195Ar 3  2A               rol
00195Br 3  18               clc
00195Cr 3  65 FD            adc TEMP6
00195Er 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
001960r 3  5A               phy
001961r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_1
001964r 3  7A               ply
001965r 3               FA_OPSKIPPUSH:
001965r 3  68               pla
001966r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
001968r 3  E6 FC            inc TEMP5
00196Ar 3  80 C6            bra FA_OPASLP
00196Cr 3               FA_OPASNOT:
00196Cr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next

0019FFr 3  A9 C7                        lda         #$C7
001A01r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001A04r 3

001A12r 3

001A40r 3               
001A40r 3  A2 61                        ldx         #$61    ; Total Level (volume) for operator 1, Channel 1

001A47r 3               
001A47r 3  A9 rr                        lda         #<SCOM_NOTES_0
001A49r 3  85 rr                        sta         YMN0L

001A51r 3  85 rr                        sta         YMN1L
001A53r 3  A9 rr                        lda         #>SCOM_NOTES_1
001A55r 3  85 rr                        sta         YMN1H
001A57r 3               
001A57r 3  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; counter for channel 0
001A59r 3               @play_loop:
001A59r 3                               ; 0. set outer counter to 0
001A59r 3                               ; 1. key off channel 0
001A59r 3                               ; 1a.  key off on channel 1
001A59r 3                               ; 2. set note to next note in channel 0 list
001A59r 3                               ; 3. key on on channel 0
001A59r 3                               ; 4. one delay
001A59r 3                               ; 5. notes for rest of sequence on channel 1
001A59r 3                               ; 5a.  reset counter to 0
001A59r 3                               ; 5b.  key off on channel 1
001A59r 3                               ; 5c.  set note on channel 1
001A59r 3                               ; 5d.  key on on channel 1
001A59r 3                               ; 5e.  one delay
001A59r 3                               ; 5f.  increment counter
001A59r 3                               ; 5g.  loop until 7 notes have been played on channel 1
001A59r 3                               ; 6.  increment outer counter to play next start note on channel 0
001A59r 3                               ; 7.  loop back to 1 until outer count reaches 8 (8 sequences have played)
001A59r 3                               ; Key Off (silence previous note)
001A59r 3               
001A59r 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
001A5Br 3  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; CH0 off
001A5Dr 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001A60r 3               
001A60r 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
001A62r 3  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
001A64r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001A67r 3               
001A67r 3                               ; Set frequency (KC)
001A67r 3  A2 28                        ldx         #$28    ; KC register for CH0
001A69r 3  B1 rr                        lda         (YMN0L),y
001A6Br 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001A6Er 3               
001A6Er 3                               ; Key On
001A6Er 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
001A70r 3  A9 78                        lda         #$78    ; CH0 on
001A72r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001A75r 3               
001A75r 3                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
001A75r 3  A9 08                        lda         #8
001A77r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
001A7Ar 3               
001A7Ar 3  5A                           phy
001A7Br 3  A0 00                        ldy         #0
001A7Dr 3  80 07                        bra         @skip_ch1_off   ; already off at the start of the loop
001A7Fr 3               
001A7Fr 3               @next_ch1:
001A7Fr 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
001A81r 3  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
001A83r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001A86r 3               
001A86r 3               @skip_ch1_off:
001A86r 3                               ; Set frequency (KC)
001A86r 3  A2 29                        ldx         #$29    ; KC register for CH1
001A88r 3  B1 rr                        lda         (YMN1L),y
001A8Ar 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001A8Dr 3               
001A8Dr 3                               ; Key On
001A8Dr 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
001A8Fr 3  A9 79                        lda         #$79    ; CH1 on
001A91r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001A94r 3               
001A94r 3                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
001A94r 3  A9 08                        lda         #8
001A96r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
001A99r 3               
001A99r 3  C8                           iny
001A9Ar 3  C0 07                        cpy         #7
001A9Cr 3  D0 E1                        bne         @next_ch1
001A9Er 3               
001A9Er 3                               ; Next note on CH0
001A9Er 3  7A                           ply                 ; pull CH0 count
001A9Fr 3  C8                           iny
001AA0r 3  C0 08                        cpy         #8      ; 8 bars
001AA2r 3  D0 B5                        bne         @play_loop

001AA4r 3                               ; Stop sound
001AA4r 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
001AA6r 3  A9 00                        lda         #$00
001AA8r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001AABr 3

001AADr 3  A9 01                        lda         #$01

001AB2r 3               ;
001AB2r 3               ;  training data
001AB2r 3               ;
001AB2r 3               ;
001AB2r 3               ;    LIBRARY header in standard format (reachable from lib_jumptable entry)
001AB2r 3               ;
001AB2r 3               ;    LT_xxxx label, followed by 'sz' string, followed by 'C' or 'B' type
001AB2r 3               ;    ..and then calculated ROM address of start of library, bigendian.
001AB2r 3               ;
001AB2r 3               ;    Library entries can be ':' definitions or regular commands.
001AB2r 3               ;
001AB2r 3               ;   the 'sys_word' macro only works with names that are 'defined' labels
001AB2r 3               ;    i.e. NOT labels for actual physical addresses.   LBLX = <number> vs LBLX:
001AB2r 3               ;
001AB2r 3               .macro sys_word name
001AB2r 3               makelabel "sv_", name
001AB2r 3                 .byte .sprintf( "%i cons s_%s", .ident (name), name )
001AB2r 3                 .byte 0
001AB2r 3               .endmacro
001AB2r 3               ;

00266Cr 3               ;  0 - A            A  -(1)
00266Cr 3               ;  1 - #            n  -(2)
00266Cr 3               ;  2 - (zp, x)      ix -(2)
00266Cr 3               ;  3 - (zp), y      iy -(2)
00266Cr 3               ;  4 - (zp)         iz -(2)
00266Cr 3               ;  5 -  zp          z  -(2)
00266Cr 3               ;  6 -  zp, x       zx -(2)
00266Cr 3               ;  7 -  zp, y       zy -(2)
00266Cr 3               ;  8 -  a, y        ay -(3)
00266Cr 3               ;  9 -  a           a  -(3)
00266Cr 3               ;  A -  a, x        ax -(3)
00266Cr 3               ;  B - indirect     i  -(3)
00266Cr 3               ;  C - indirect     ia -(3)
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;------------------
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;   8 opcode FAMILY tables.  **NOTE*** all subtables end with $03, which is not a valid opcode.
00266Cr 3               ;    $03 is used to detect end of one table and switch to next.
00266Cr 3               ;    $03, $03 is end of table.
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;   14 x 14 bytes addressing mode / size table, so line terminations unnecessary
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;   addr mode 0 is 1 byte, 1 to 7 are 2 bytes, 8 to C are 3 bytes
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;   sng1, sng3 have none or one address mode
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;   A, n, ix, iy, iz, z, zx, zy, ay, a, ax, i, ia are all SUFFIX's used on opcodes...so
00266Cr 3               ;     e.g. instead of LDA ($XXXX),y  you will say 'LDAiy XXXX'.
00266Cr 3               ;   Other examples
00266Cr 3               ;     INC A ==> INCA
00266Cr 3               ;     BIT #$45 ==> BITn 45
00266Cr 3               ;     JMP (:00),x will be JMPia :00
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;     but...BCC :B3 ==> BCC :B3  (before offset calculations)
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;   ..and so on.
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;    FULL PROGRAM EXAMPLE
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;    .org 800
00266Cr 3               ;    :80 F803   ( WRITE_CHAR )
00266Cr 3               ;    :81 F0     ( TEMP1 )
00266Cr 3               ;    :82 F1     ( TEMP1+1 )
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;    JSR :00                 ( jump to start )
00266Cr 3               ;    :01
00266Cr 3               ;    .byte 41 42 43 44 00    (ABCD, zero term'd)
00266Cr 3               ;    :00
00266Cr 3               ;    LDAn <:01             ( top byte of address)
00266Cr 3               ;    STAz :81               ( TEMP1 in Forth )
00266Cr 3               ;    LDAn >:01             ( bottom byte)
00266Cr 3               ;    STAz :82              ( TEMP1+1 in Forth )
00266Cr 3               ;    LDYn 00
00266Cr 3               ;    :02                   ( top of loop )
00266Cr 3               ;    LDAiy F0
00266Cr 3               ;    JSR :80               ( print character )
00266Cr 3               ;    BNE :02               ( branch to top of loop )
00266Cr 3               ;    RTS
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ; ----------------------------
00266Cr 3               ;   So...in HyForth itself you'd do something like this after startup:
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;  HF> 10 lib autoload  ( load FASM library )
00266Cr 3               ;  HF> feclr      ( clear screen buffer )
00266Cr 3               ;  HF> fa+ .org 800
00266Cr 3               ;  HF> fa+ :80 F803 ( WRITE CHAR )
00266Cr 3               ;  ....
00266Cr 3               ;  HF> fa+ RTS
00266Cr 3               ;  HF> faasm      ( well, just do it, store in buffer )
00266Cr 3               ;  HF> faload     (  put it in memory from buffer )
00266Cr 3               ;  HF> $800 1 disasm
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;  [ DISASM output of page at $800 ]
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;   A bit Forthy and astringent, I agree, but....
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;   PROCEDURE:
00266Cr 3               ;      lookup mnemonic in MN_STRING to find offset table value
00266Cr 3               ;      lookup offset value in MN_OFFSETS to get 'base' mnemonic op code
00266Cr 3               ;      lookup 'base' code from MN_OFFSETS table in FAMILY tables FA_sng1, sng3, mb, br, tba, mma, mmb
00266Cr 3               ;         if not there, look through first byte of addr sequence (STZ thru JMP)
00266Cr 3               ;         this will give you the 'base family' code
00266Cr 3               ;      SAVE base family code appearing in table where found
00266Cr 3               ;      lookup address mode in corresponding row using base family code
00266Cr 3               ;      ADD value in that row/column to mnemonic 'base' opcode (not base family code)
00266Cr 3               ;      This should be correct OPCODE
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;      BYTES:  table sng1 and address mode 'A' (index 0) are single byte codes
00266Cr 3               ;              tables mb, br, and modes 1 thru 7 are 2 byte codes
00266Cr 3               ;              table sng3 and modes 8 thru C are 3 byte codes
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;      BRANCHES:  note when opcode found and mark 'relative' byte with $00 or $FF until
00266Cr 3               ;            label offset calculated and filled in.  BBR / BBS opcodes will also have to be adjusted
00266Cr 3               ;            based on what digit follows mnemonic, e.g. (BBR lookup yields $0F, BBR6 requires
00266Cr 3               ;            adding $60 to $0F...)
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;      RMB/SMB:  these OPCODES will also have to be adjusted based on following digit
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               ;--------------------- 34
00266Cr 3               ;                           SZ TO ADDESS MODE
00266Cr 3               ;
00266Cr 3               FA_amtable1 = FA_xamt1 + COPYSTART - RAMSTART
00266Cr 3               FA_amtable2 = FA_xamt2 + COPYSTART - RAMSTART
00266Cr 3               FA_ops = FA_xops + COPYSTART - RAMSTART
00266Cr 3               FA_addr = FA_xaddr + COPYSTART - RAMSTART
00266Cr 3               FA_anofam = FA_xopnofam + COPYSTART - RAMSTART
00266Cr 3               
00266Cr 3               FA_xamtablen1 = 10
00266Cr 3               FA_xamtablen2 = 24
00266Cr 3               FA_xamt1:
00266Cr 3  41 00            .byte $41,0        ; A
00266Er 3  61 09            .byte $61,9        ; a
002670r 3  69 0B            .byte $69,11       ; i
002672r 3  6E 01            .byte $6E,1        ; n
002674r 3  7A 05            .byte $7A,5        ; z
002676r 3               FA_xamt2:
002676r 3  61 78 0A         .byte $61,$78,10   ; ax
002679r 3  61 79 08         .byte $61,$79,8    ; ay
00267Cr 3  69 61 0C         .byte $69,$61,12   ; ia
00267Fr 3  69 78 02         .byte $69,$78,2    ; ix
002682r 3  69 79 03         .byte $69,$79,3    ; iy
002685r 3  69 7A 04         .byte $69,$7A,4    ; iz
002688r 3  7A 78 06         .byte $7A,$78,6    ; zx
00268Br 3  7A 79 07         .byte $7A,$79,7    ; zy
00268Er 3               ;--------------------- 49
00268Er 3               ;                               OPCODE FAMILY
00268Er 3               ;
00268Er 3               FA_FAMCNT = 6
00268Er 3               FA_xops:
00268Er 3               FA_sng1:                                         ; all single byte instructions
00268Er 3  00 08 18 28   .byte $00, $08, $18, $28, $38, $40, $48
002692r 3  38 40 48     
002695r 3  58 5A 60 68   .byte $58, $5A, $60, $68, $78, $7A, $88, $8A, $98, $9A
002699r 3  78 7A 88 8A  
00269Dr 3  98 9A        
00269Fr 3  A8 AA B8 BA   .byte $A8, $AA, $B8, $BA, $C8, $CA, $CB, $D8, $DA, $DB, $E8, $EA, $F8, $FA, 3
0026A3r 3  C8 CA CB D8  
0026A7r 3  DA DB E8 EA  
0026AEr 3  20 03        FA_sng3:  .byte $20, 3                           ; JSR  3 bytes
0026B0r 3               ;FA_mb:  .byte $07, $87, 3                        ; RMB, SMB  2 bytes
0026B0r 3               FA_br:
0026B0r 3  0F 10 30 50   .byte $0F, $10, $30, $50, $70                   ; all branch instructions
0026B4r 3  70           
0026B5r 3  80 8F 90 B0   .byte $80, $8F, $90, $B0, $D0, $F0, 3           ; 2 bytes
0026B9r 3  D0 F0 03     
0026BCr 3               ;------------------
0026BCr 3               ;   below have multiple address modes
0026BCr 3               ;
0026BCr 3               ;-------------------- 18
0026BCr 3               FA_tba:                                          ; TBR, TBS
0026BCr 3  04 14 03      .byte $04, $14, 3
0026BFr 3               FA_mma:                                          ; ORA, AND, EOR, ADC, STA, LDA, CMP, SBC
0026BFr 3  01 21 41 61   .byte $01, $21, $41, $61, $81, $A1, $C1, $E1, 3
0026C3r 3  81 A1 C1 E1  
0026C7r 3  03           
0026C8r 3               FA_mmb:                                          ; ASL, ROL, LSR, ROR
0026C8r 3  06 26 46 66   .byte $06, $26, $46, $66, 3
0026CCr 3  03           
0026CDr 3               FA_opend:
0026CDr 3  03            .byte 3
0026CEr 3               ;------------------- 196
0026CEr 3               ;                              ADDRESS MODES
0026CEr 3               ;
0026CEr 3               ;       op   A    n    ix   iy   iz   z   zx   zy    ay   a   ax   i    ia
0026CEr 3               FA_AMRLEN = 14
0026CEr 3               FA_xaddr:
0026CEr 3               FA_tbaa:
0026CEr 3  04 FF FF FF   .byte $04, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $00, $FF, $FF, $FF, $08, $FF, $FF, $FF
0026D2r 3  FF FF 00 FF  
0026D6r 3  FF FF 08 FF  
0026DCr 3               FA_mmaa:
0026DCr 3  01 FF 08 00   .byte $01, $FF, $08, $00, $10, $11, $04, $14, $FF, $18, $0C, $1C, $FF, $FF
0026E0r 3  10 11 04 14  
0026E4r 3  FF 18 0C 1C  
0026EAr 3               FA_mmba:
0026EAr 3  06 04 FF FF   .byte $06, $04, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $00, $10, $FF, $FF, $08, $18, $FF, $FF
0026EEr 3  FF FF 00 10  
0026F2r 3  FF FF 08 18  
0026F8r 3               FA_xopnofam:
0026F8r 3               FA_STZ:
0026F8r 3  64 FF FF FF   .byte $64, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $00, $10, $FF, $FF, $38, $3A, $FF, $FF
0026FCr 3  FF FF 00 10  
002700r 3  FF FF 38 3A  
002706r 3               FA_LDX:
002706r 3  A2 FF 00 FF   .byte $A2, $FF, $00, $FF, $FF, $FF, $04, $FF, $14, $1C, $0C, $FF, $FF, $FF
00270Ar 3  FF FF 04 FF  
00270Er 3  14 1C 0C FF  
002714r 3               FA_LDY:
002714r 3  A0 FF 00 FF   .byte $A0, $FF, $00, $FF, $FF, $FF, $04, $14, $FF, $FF, $0C, $1C, $FF, $FF
002718r 3  FF FF 04 14  
00271Cr 3  FF FF 0C 1C  
002722r 3               FA_CPX:
002722r 3  E0 FF FF FF   .byte $E0, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $04, $FF, $FF, $FF, $0C, $FF, $FF, $FF
002726r 3  FF FF 04 FF  
00272Ar 3  FF FF 0C FF  
002730r 3               FA_CPY:
002730r 3  C0 FF FF FF   .byte $C0, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $04, $FF, $FF, $FF, $0C, $FF, $FF, $FF
002734r 3  FF FF 04 FF  
002738r 3  FF FF 0C FF  
00273Er 3               FA_STX:
00273Er 3  86 FF FF FF   .byte $86, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $00, $FF, $10, $FF, $08, $FF, $FF, $FF
002742r 3  FF FF 00 FF  
002746r 3  10 FF 08 FF  
00274Cr 3               FA_STY:
00274Cr 3  84 FF FF FF   .byte $84, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $00, $10, $FF, $FF, $08, $FF, $FF, $FF
002750r 3  FF FF 00 10  
002754r 3  FF FF 08 FF  
00275Ar 3               FA_INC:
00275Ar 3  1A 00 FF FF   .byte $1A, $00, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $CC, $DC, $FF, $FF, $D4, $E4, $FF, $FF
00275Er 3  FF FF CC DC  
002762r 3  FF FF D4 E4  
002768r 3               FA_DEC:
002768r 3  3A 00 FF FF   .byte $3A, $00, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $8C, $9C, $FF, $FF, $94, $A4, $FF, $FF
00276Cr 3  FF FF 8C 9C  
002770r 3  FF FF 94 A4

00277Ar 3  FF FF 00 10  
00277Er 3  FF FF 08 18  
002784r 3               FA_JMP:
002784r 3  4C FF FF FF   .byte $4C, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $FF, $00, $FF, $20, $30
002788r 3  FF FF FF FF  
00278Cr 3  FF FF 00 FF  
002792r 3  00            .byte 0     ; backstop at end

002793r 2               ;
002793r 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
002793r 2               ;                            BINARY LIBRARIES
002793r 2               ;
002793r 2               .include "bload.s"
002793r 3               ;
002793r 3               ;  BLOAD test code
002793r 3               ;
002793r 3               ;  ALL binary libraries loadable with 'bload' MUST be Forth words,
002793r 3               ;   (not jsr subroutines or jmp destinations), fully relocatable.  That means NO direct jmp/jsr's
002793r 3               ;   to internal labels.  Indirect jmps ($6C / $7C) to local locations can be used
002793r 3               ;   once the word is loaded, with smart code.   Branches will work fine.
002793r 3               ;
002793r 3               ;   jsr's and jmp's to core primitive labels (next, keeps, this, etc) or
002793r 3               ;   OS ROM ($E000 - $FFFF) locations will also work.
002793r 3               ;
002793r 3               ;   These words will be loaded into the RAM heap, so there CAN be self-modding
002793r 3               ;   sections that will help words to configure themselves.  Be creative!
002793r 3               ;
002793r 3               ;  Format below must be rigidly followed, with length of name followed
002793r 3               ;   by that many bytes for the name.  'bit #0' ($89 $00) or $22 $00 (undocumented)
002793r 3               ;   must be the first instruction in any native code word loaded past
002793r 3               ;   'exit'.  Termination must be $00 $00 for each word, with "END"
002793r 3               ;   to designate the end of a 'library'.
002793r 3               ;
002793r 3               ;    LIBRARY header in standard format (reachable from lib_jumptable entry)
002793r 3               ;
002793r 3               ;    LT_xxxx label, followed by 'sz' string, followed by 'C' or 'B' type
002793r 3               ;    ..and then calculated ROM address of start of first loadable word, bigendian.
002793r 3               ;
002793r 3               LT_BLOAD:
002793r 3  42 4C 4F 41  .byte "BLOAD"
002797r 3  44           
002798r 3  00           .byte 0
002799r 3  42           .byte "B"
00279Ar 3  C8           .byte >(bload0 + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
00279Br 3  9C           .byte <(bload0 + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
00279Cr 3               bload0:
00279Cr 3               ;
00279Cr 3  06           .byte 6           ; length of name
00279Dr 3  42 4C 74 65  .byte "BLtest"
0027A1r 3  73 74        
0027A3r 3  22                .byte $22
0027A4r 3  00                .byte 0
0027A5r 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
0027A9r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
0027ADr 3  20 rr rr 68  
0027B1r 3  A9 54             lda #'T'
0027B3r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
0027B6r 3  A9 45             lda  #'E'
0027B8r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
0027BBr 3  A9 53             lda  #'S'
0027BDr 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
0027C0r 3  A9 54             lda  #'T'
0027C2r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
0027C5r 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
0027C9r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
0027CDr 3  20 rr rr 68  
0027D1r 3  4C rr rr          jmp next
0027D4r 3  00 00        .byte 0, 0
0027D6r 3  07           .byte 7           ; length of name
0027D7r 3  42 4C 74 65  .byte "BLtest2"
0027DBr 3  73 74 32     
0027DEr 3  22                .byte $22
0027DFr 3  00                .byte 0
0027E0r 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
0027E4r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
0027E8r 3  20 rr rr 68  
0027ECr 3  A9 54             lda #'T'
0027EEr 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
0027F1r 3  A9 45             lda #'E'
0027F3r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
0027F6r 3  A9 53             lda #'S'
0027F8r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
0027FBr 3  A9 54             lda #'T'
0027FDr 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
002800r 3  A9 59             lda #'Y'
002802r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
002805r 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
002809r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
00280Dr 3  20 rr rr 68  
002811r 3  4C rr rr          jmp next
002814r 3  00 00        .byte 0, 0
002816r 3  45 4E 44     .byte "END"                  ; required to end library!
002819r 3               bload0_end:

004000r 2               ;
004000r 2               
004000r 1               .include "wozmon.s"
004000r 2               ;
004000r 2               ;   Taken mostly from Steve Wozniak's Apple 1 Monitor for the 6502, or WOZMON
004000r 2               ;
004000r 2               .debuginfo
004000r 2               ;
004000r 2               ;          No longer here; renamed WOZ_IN, moved to bios.s
004000r 2               ;
004000r 2               ;.segment "BUFFERS"
004000r 2               ;IN:
004000r 2               ;                .res            $100
004000r 2               ;
004000r 2               .segment "WOZMON"
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; usually ends up at $FE00  (see os_rom_C02.cfg)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; WOZMON Entrypoint
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               MON_START:
000000r 2  D8                           cld                             ; Clear decimal arithmetic mode.
000001r 2  58                           cli                             ; Enable interrupts
000002r 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_D_STATE      ; Byte mode, not DISAM mode
000004r 2  80 0B                        bra             @is_start
000006r 2               
000006r 2               @not_cr:
000006r 2  C9 08                        cmp             #$08            ; backspace
000008r 2  F0 16                        beq             @is_backspace
00000Ar 2  C9 1B                        cmp             #$1B            ; escape
00000Cr 2  F0 03                        beq             @is_start
00000Er 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
00000Fr 2  10 12                        bpl             @get_next_char  ; Auto ESC if line longer than 127.
000011r 2               
000011r 2               @is_start:
000011r 2  A9 0D                        lda #$0D
000013r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
000016r 2  A9 0A                        lda #$0A
000018r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
00001Br 2               
00001Br 2               @get_line:
00001Br 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_PROMPT
00001Er 2  A0 01                        ldy             #1              ; Initialize text index.
000020r 2               
000020r 2               @is_backspace:
000020r 2  88                           dey                             ; Back up text index.
000021r 2  30 F8                        bmi             @get_line       ; Beyond start of line, reinitialize.
000023r 2               
000023r 2               @get_next_char:
000023r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             READ_CHAR
000026r 2  90 FB                        bcc             @get_next_char
000028r 2  99 rr rr                     sta             WOZ_IN,y            ; Add to text buffer.
00002Br 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #$0D
00002Dr 2  D0 D7                        bne             @not_cr
00002Fr 2  A0 FF                        ldy             #$FF            ; Reset text index.  Will iny shortly...
000031r 2  A9 00                        lda             #$00            ; For ZP_WM_XAM mode.
000033r 2  AA                           tax                             ; .X=0.
000034r 2               
000034r 2               @set_block:
000034r 2  0A                           asl
000035r 2               
000035r 2               @set_store:
000035r 2  0A                           asl                             ; Leaves $7B if setting STOR mode.
000036r 2               
000036r 2               @set_mode:
000036r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_MODE      ; $00 = ZP_WM_XAM, $74 = STOR, $B8 = BLOK ZP_WM_XAM.
000038r 2               
000038r 2               @skip_delim:
000038r 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
000039r 2               
000039r 2               @next_item:
000039r 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             WOZ_IN,y            ; Get character.
00003Cr 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #$0D         ; CR?
00003Er 2  F0 DB                        beq             @get_line       ; Yes, done with this line.
000040r 2  C9 2E                        cmp             #'.'
000042r 2  90 F4                        bcc             @skip_delim     ; Skip delimiter.
000044r 2  F0 EE                        beq             @set_block      ; Set BLOCK ZP_WM_XAM mode.
000046r 2  C9 3A                        cmp             #':'
000048r 2  F0 EB                        beq             @set_store      ; Yes, set STOR mode.
00004Ar 2  C9 4D                        cmp             #'M'
00004Cr 2  10 0A                        bpl             @check_rtow
00004Er 2  C9 4B                        cmp             #'K'
000050r 2  30 15                        bmi             @nothing
000052r 2  49 4B                        eor             #'K'         ; 'K' resets DASTATE to 0
000054r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_D_STATE
000056r 2  80 E0                        bra             @skip_delim
000058r 2               
000058r 2               @check_rtow:
000058r 2  C9 52                        cmp             #'R'
00005Ar 2  F0 45                        beq             @run_prog
00005Cr 2  C9 5E                        cmp     #'^'
00005Er 2  F0 02                        beq     @go_rts
000060r 2  80 05                        bra     @nothing
000062r 2               @go_rts:
000062r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp     WOZRTS
000065r 2               
000065r 2               @bra_is_start:
000065r 2  80 AA                        bra             @is_start       ; can't branch from top to botthom w/o this
000067r 2               
000067r 2               ;@not_tuvw:
000067r 2               @nothing:
000067r 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Save Y for comparison
000069r 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP       ; $00 -> Low byte of HPV
00006Br 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; ...and High byte.
00006Dr 2               
00006Dr 2               @next_hex:
00006Dr 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             WOZ_IN,y            ; Get character for hex test.
000070r 2  49 30                        eor             #'0'            ; Map digits to $0-9.
000072r 2  C9 0A                        cmp             #$0A            ; Digit?
000074r 2  90 06                        bcc             @is_digit       ; Yes.
000076r 2  69 88                        adc             #$88            ; Map letter "A"-"F" to $FA-FF.
000078r 2  C9 FA                        cmp             #$FA            ; Hex letter?
00007Ar 2  90 11                        bcc             @not_hex        ; No, character not hex.
00007Cr 2               
00007Cr 2               @is_digit:
00007Cr 2  0A                           asl                             ; LSD to MSD of A.
00007Dr 2  0A                           asl
00007Er 2  0A                           asl
00007Fr 2  0A                           asl
000080r 2  A2 04                        ldx              #4              ; Shift count.
000082r 2               
000082r 2               @hex_shift:
000082r 2  0A                           asl                             ; Hex digit left, MSB to carry.
000083r 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Rotate into LSD.
000085r 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; Rotate into MSD's.
000087r 2  CA                           dex                             ; Done 4 shifts?
000088r 2  D0 F8                        bne             @hex_shift      ; No, loop.
00008Ar 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
00008Br 2  D0 E0                        bne             @next_hex       ; Always taken. Check next character for hex.
00008Dr 2               
00008Dr 2               @not_hex:
00008Dr 2  C4 rr                        cpy             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Check if HPV empty (no hex digits).
00008Fr 2  F0 D4                        beq             @bra_is_start   ; Yes, back to start.
000091r 2               
000091r 2               @not_hex_or_escape:
000091r 2  24 rr                        bit             ZP_WM_MODE      ; Test ZP_WM_MODE byte.
000093r 2  50 15                        bvc             @not_store      ; B6=0 is STOR, 1 is XAM and BLOCK XAM.
000095r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP       ; LSD's of hex data.
000097r 2  92 rr                        sta             (ZP_WM_ST)      ; Store to current 'store index'.
000099r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST        ; Increment store index.
00009Br 2  D0 9C                        bne             @next_item      ; Get next item (no carry).
00009Dr 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST + 1    ; Add carry to 'store index' high order.
00009Fr 2               
00009Fr 2               @to_next_item:
00009Fr 2  80 98                        bra             @next_item      ; Get next command item.
0000A1r 2               
0000A1r 2               @run_prog:
0000A1r 2  A9 rr                        lda             #>(MON_START-1)   ; push Wozmon entry point on stack
0000A3r 2  48                           pha
0000A4r 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<(MON_START-1)
0000A6r 2  48                           pha
0000A7r 2  6C rr rr                     jmp             (ZP_WM_XAM)       ; then jump to program
0000AAr 2               @not_store:
0000AAr 2  30 29                        bmi             @examine_next   ; B7 = 0 for XAM, 1 for BLOCK XAM.
0000ACr 2  A2 02                        ldx             #2              ; Byte count.
0000AEr 2               
0000AEr 2               @set_addr:
0000AEr 2  B5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP - 1,x ; Copy hex data to
0000B0r 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_ST - 1,x  ;   'store index'.
0000B2r 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM - 1,x ; And to 'ZP_WM_XAM index'.
0000B4r 2  CA                           dex                             ; Next of 2 bytes.
0000B5r 2  D0 F7                        bne             @set_addr       ; Loop unless X = 0.
0000B7r 2               
0000B7r 2               @print_next_addr:
0000B7r 2  A9 0D                        lda #$0D
0000B9r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
0000BCr 2  A9 0A                        lda #$0A
0000BEr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
0000C1r 2  A5 rr                        lda ZP_WM_XAM+1
0000C3r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_BYTE
0000C6r 2  A5 rr                        lda ZP_WM_XAM                    ; Print 'examine index'
0000C8r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_BYTE
0000CBr 2  A9 3A                        lda #':'
0000CDr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR                   ; ...+ COLON
0000D0r 2               @print_data:
0000D0r 2  5A                           phy
0000D1r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             DISASM_WM       ; DISASM
0000D4r 2  7A                           ply
0000D5r 2               
0000D5r 2               @examine_next:
0000D5r 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_WM_MODE      ; 0 -> ZP_WM_MODE (XAM mode).
0000D7r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM
0000D9r 2  C5 rr                        cmp             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Compare 'examine index' to hex data.
0000DBr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000DDr 2  E5 rr                        sbc             ZP_WM_HVP + 1
0000DFr 2  B0 BE                        bcs             @to_next_item   ; Not less, so no more data to output.
0000E1r 2  38                           sec
0000E2r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_EXBYTES
0000E4r 2  65 rr                        adc             ZP_WM_XAM
0000E6r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM
0000E8r 2  90 02                        bcc             :+
0000EAr 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000ECr 2               :
0000ECr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_STATE      ; if disassembling, always print the address
0000EEr 2  D0 C7                        bne             @print_next_addr
0000F0r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM       ; Check low-order 'examine index' byte
0000F2r 2  29 07                        and             #7              ; For MOD 8 = 0
0000F4r 2  F0 C1                        beq             @print_next_addr
0000F6r 2  80 D8                        bra             @print_data
0000F8r 2               WOZRTS:
0000F8r 2  60                           rts                  ; return to BASIC or Forth
0000F9r 2               ;
0000F9r 2               ;          end of wozmon.s
0000F9r 2               ;
0000F9r 2               
0000F9r 1               ;.include "mmu.s"
0000F9r 1               .include "shell.s"
0000F9r 2               .debuginfo
0000F9r 2               .segment "SHELL"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SHELL_MAIN:
000000r 2  20 rr rr                  jsr COPYTORAM                  ; COPYTORAM
000003r 2  4C rr rr                  jmp RAMSTART                   ; forth_main -> F_cold
000006r 2                ;           jsr     MON_START
