000006r 1               .include "utes.s"
000006r 2               ;
000006r 2               ;  utes.s        -   math routines, Forth/OS utilities, sound init/core
000006r 2               ;
000006r 2               .debuginfo
000006r 2               .segment "UTES"
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; usually ends up at $E400 (see os_rom_C02.cfg)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
000000r 2               ;SOUND_INIT, YM_LOADPATCH, YM_WRITE
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; zero out all YM-2151 registers $28-$FF
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               SOUND_INIT:
000000r 2  48                           pha
000001r 2  DA                           phx
000002r 2  A9 00                        lda         #0
000004r 2  A2 14                        ldx         #$14        ; turn off the clocks
000006r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000009r 2  A2 28                        ldx         #$28
00000Br 2               
00000Br 2               @write_z:
00000Br 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00000Er 2  B0 0D                        bcs         @error
000010r 2  E8                           inx
000011r 2  D0 F8                        bne         @write_z
000013r 2  A2 03                        ldx         #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SOUND
000015r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000017r 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000019r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         IRQ_SET_VECTOR
00001Cr 2  18                           clc
00001Dr 2               
00001Dr 2               @error:
00001Dr 2  FA                           plx
00001Er 2  68                           pla
00001Fr 2  60                           rts
000020r 2               
000020r 2               
000020r 2               SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER:
000020r 2                               ; check which
000020r 2  40                           rti
000021r 2               ;
000021r 2               ;         zero page stuff for sound, in zero.s
000021r 2               ;YMN0L = ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000021r 2               ;YMN0H = YMN0L + 1
000021r 2               ;YMN1L = ZP_TEMP_VEC4
000021r 2               ;YMN1H = YMN1L + 1
000021r 2               ;
000021r 2               ;AZP0L = ZP_TEMP_VEC
000021r 2               ;AZP0H = AZP0L + 1
000021r 2               ;YMTMP1 = ZP_TEMP_VEC2
000021r 2               ;YMTMP2 = YMTMP1 + 1
000021r 2               ;
000021r 2               YM_TIMEOUT = 64
000021r 2               
000021r 2               ; Write value in A to YM-2151 register in X
000021r 2               YM_WRITE:
000021r 2  78                           sei
000022r 2  5A                           phy
000023r 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
000025r 2               
000025r 2               @ym_wait1:
000025r 2  88                           dey
000026r 2  30 18                        bmi         @timeout
000028r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
00002Br 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait1
00002Dr 2  8E rr rr                     stx         YM_REG
000030r 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
000032r 2               
000032r 2               @ym_wait2:
000032r 2  88                           dey
000033r 2  30 0B                        bmi         @timeout
000035r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
000038r 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait2
00003Ar 2  8D rr rr                     sta         YM_DATA
00003Dr 2  18                           clc
00003Er 2  80 01                        bra         @cleanup
000040r 2               
000040r 2               @timeout:
000040r 2  38                           sec
000041r 2               
000041r 2               @cleanup:
000041r 2  7A                           ply
000042r 2  58                           cli
000043r 2  60                           rts
000044r 2               
000044r 2               YM_LOADPATCH:
000044r 2                               ; Make re-entrant safe by protecting tmp and pointer variables from interrupt
000044r 2  08                           php
000045r 2  78                           sei
000046r 2               
000046r 2                               ; and #$07 ; mask channel to range 0..7
000046r 2  86 rr                        stx AZP0L
000048r 2  84 rr                        sty AZP0H
00004Ar 2  18                           clc
00004Br 2  69 20                        adc #$20 ; first byte of patch goes to YM:$20+channel
00004Dr 2  AA                           tax
00004Er 2               
00004Er 2  B2 rr                        lda (AZP0L)
000050r 2  29 3F                        and #$3F
000052r 2               
000052r 2                               ;sta ymtmp1
000052r 2                               ;lda ymshadow,x
000052r 2                               ;and #$C0 ; L+R bits for YM channel
000052r 2                               ;ora ymtmp1 ; Add the patch byte without L+R
000052r 2  09 C0                        ora #$C0;
000054r 2               
000054r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
000057r 2  B0 16                        bcs @fail
000059r 2  A0 00                        ldy #0
00005Br 2  8A                           txa         ; YM_WRITE preserves X (YM register)
00005Cr 2                               ; Now skip over $28 and $30 by adding $10 to the register address.
00005Cr 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful ym_write
00005Cr 2  69 10                        adc #$10
00005Er 2  AA                           tax         ; set up for loop
00005Fr 2               @next:
00005Fr 2  8A                           txa
000060r 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful YM_WRITE
000060r 2  69 08                        adc #$08
000062r 2  B0 0E                        bcs @success
000064r 2  C8                           iny
000065r 2  AA                           tax
000066r 2  B1 rr                        lda (AZP0L),y
000068r 2  5A                           phy         ; YM_WRITE clobbers .Y
000069r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
00006Cr 2  7A                           ply
00006Dr 2  90 F0                        bcc @next
00006Fr 2               @fail:
00006Fr 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
000070r 2  38                           sec
000071r 2  60                           rts         ; return C set as failed patch write.
000072r 2               @success:
000072r 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
000073r 2  18                           clc
000074r 2  60                           rts
000075r 2               
000075r 2               ; YM_DELAY_64 - Delay subroutine for musical note lengths
000075r 2               ; A: number of 64th-note delays to wait (a 64th note @120 BPM = ~ 111,861 clock cycles @ 3.57955 MHz)
000075r 2               YM_DELAY_64:
000075r 2  DA                           phx
000076r 2  5A                           phy
000077r 2  85 rr                        sta         YMTMP1
000079r 2               
000079r 2               @far_outer:
000079r 2  A9 01                        lda         #1          ; 2 cycles
00007Br 2  A2 4B                        ldx         #75
00007Dr 2  A0 8E                        ldy         #142
00007Fr 2  80 04                        bra         @inner
000081r 2               
000081r 2               @outer:
000081r 2  A2 00                        ldx         #0          ; 2 cycles
000083r 2               
000083r 2               @mid:
000083r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; 2 cycles
000085r 2               
000085r 2               @inner:
000085r 2  88                           dey                     ; 2 cycles
000086r 2  D0 FD                        bne         @inner      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000088r 2  CA                           dex                     ; 2 cycles
000089r 2  D0 F8                        bne         @mid        ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
00008Br 2               
00008Br 2                               ; Fine-tuning at end of inner loop
00008Br 2  3A                           dec                     ; 2 cycles
00008Cr 2  D0 F3                        bne         @outer      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
00008Er 2  C6 rr                        dec         YMTMP1      ; 3 cycles
000090r 2  D0 E7                        bne         @far_outer  ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000092r 2  7A                           ply
000093r 2  FA                           plx
000094r 2  60                           rts                     ; 6 cycles
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;  end of sound utilities
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
000095r 2               ;                MATH routines
000095r 2               ;         with MULT16 / DIV16, signs handled by calling word.
000095r 2               ;         we just do the math here.
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;    transition to Z_MATH1,2,3,4 to replace use of TEMP1,2,3,4
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               MULT16:                             ; 16 x 16 multiply; Z_MATH1 and Z_MATH2 are #'s, Z_MATH1 will be result
000095r 2  64 B6            stz Z_MATH3                       ; with Z_MATH3 as high bytes
000097r 2  64 B7            stz Z_MATH3+1
000099r 2  A2 11            ldx #17
00009Br 2  18               clc
00009Cr 2               MULTLOOP:
00009Cr 2  66 B7            ror Z_MATH3+1                     ; RIGHT.  if you need to go backwards, go backwards stupid fuck.
00009Er 2  66 B6            ror Z_MATH3
0000A0r 2  66 B3            ror Z_MATH1+1
0000A2r 2  66 B2            ror Z_MATH1
0000A4r 2  90 0D            bcc MULTDECCNT
0000A6r 2  18               clc
0000A7r 2  A5 B4            lda Z_MATH2
0000A9r 2  65 B6            adc Z_MATH3
0000ABr 2  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
0000ADr 2  A5 B5            lda Z_MATH2+1
0000AFr 2  65 B7            adc Z_MATH3+1
0000B1r 2  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
0000B3r 2               MULTDECCNT:
0000B3r 2  CA               dex
0000B4r 2  D0 E6            bne MULTLOOP
0000B6r 2  60               rts
0000B7r 2               
0000B7r 2               ;
0000B7r 2               ;
0000B7r 2               ;           16 / 16 divide; Z_MATH1 and Z_MATH2 #'s - Z_MATH3 is 'overflow'
0000B7r 2               ;           Z_MATH2 divisor, Z_MATH1 dividend, Z_MATH1 + 3 = result + remainder
0000B7r 2               DIV16:
0000B7r 2  64 B6            stz Z_MATH3
0000B9r 2  64 B7            stz Z_MATH3+1
0000BBr 2  A2 10            ldx #16
0000BDr 2               UDIVLP:
0000BDr 2  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
0000BFr 2  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
0000C1r 2  26 B6            rol Z_MATH3
0000C3r 2  26 B7            rol Z_MATH3+1
0000C5r 2               UDIVCHK:
0000C5r 2  38               sec
0000C6r 2  A5 B6            lda Z_MATH3
0000C8r 2  E5 B4            sbc Z_MATH2
0000CAr 2  A8               tay
0000CBr 2  A5 B7            lda Z_MATH3+1
0000CDr 2  E5 B5            sbc Z_MATH2+1
0000CFr 2  90 04            bcc UDIVCNT
0000D1r 2  84 B6            sty Z_MATH3
0000D3r 2  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
0000D5r 2               UDIVCNT:
0000D5r 2  CA               dex
0000D6r 2  D0 E5            bne UDIVLP
0000D8r 2  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
0000DAr 2  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
0000DCr 2  60               rts
0000DDr 2               ;
0000DDr 2               ;        32 / 16 divide; Z_MATH2-Z_MATH1 / Z_MATH3 #'s - Z_MATH4 is 'overflow'
0000DDr 2               ;        -> Z_MATH2-Z_MATH1 + Z_MATH4 = result + remainder
0000DDr 2               DIV32:
0000DDr 2  64 B8            stz Z_MATH4
0000DFr 2  64 B9            stz Z_MATH4+1
0000E1r 2  A2 10            ldx #16
0000E3r 2               UDIV32LP:
0000E3r 2  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
0000E5r 2  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
0000E7r 2  26 B4            rol Z_MATH2
0000E9r 2  26 B5            rol Z_MATH2+1
0000EBr 2  26 B8            rol Z_MATH4
0000EDr 2  26 B9            rol Z_MATH4+1
0000EFr 2               UDIV32CHK:
0000EFr 2  38               sec
0000F0r 2  A5 B8            lda Z_MATH4
0000F2r 2  E5 B6            sbc Z_MATH3
0000F4r 2  A8               tay
0000F5r 2  A5 B9            lda Z_MATH4+1
0000F7r 2  E5 B7            sbc Z_MATH3+1
0000F9r 2  90 04            bcc UDIV32CNT
0000FBr 2  84 B8            sty Z_MATH4
0000FDr 2  85 B9            sta Z_MATH4+1
0000FFr 2               UDIV32CNT:
0000FFr 2  CA               dex
000100r 2  D0 E1            bne UDIV32LP
000102r 2  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
000104r 2  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
000106r 2  26 B4            rol Z_MATH2
000108r 2  26 B5            rol Z_MATH2+1
00010Ar 2  60               rts
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               ;     galois32o - LSFR psuedo-random # generator
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               ;  -- boilerplate --
00010Br 2               ; 6502 LFSR PRNG - 32-bit
00010Br 2               ; Brad Smith, 2019
00010Br 2               ; http://rainwarrior.ca
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               galois32o:
00010Br 2                   ; rotate the middle bytes left
00010Br 2  A4 DD            ldy RSEED+2                     ; will move to RSEED+3 at the end
00010Dr 2  A5 DC            lda RSEED+1
00010Fr 2  85 DD            sta RSEED+2
000111r 2                   ; compute RSEED+1 ($C5>>1 = %1100010)
000111r 2  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
000113r 2  4A               lsr
000114r 2  85 DC            sta RSEED+1                     ; reverse: 100011
000116r 2  4A               lsr
000117r 2  4A               lsr
000118r 2  4A               lsr
000119r 2  4A               lsr
00011Ar 2  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
00011Cr 2  4A               lsr
00011Dr 2  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
00011Fr 2  45 DB            eor RSEED+0                     ; combine with original low byte
000121r 2  85 DC            sta RSEED+1
000123r 2                   ; compute RSEED+0 ($C5 = %11000101)
000123r 2  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
000125r 2  0A               asl
000126r 2  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000128r 2  0A               asl
000129r 2  0A               asl
00012Ar 2  0A               asl
00012Br 2  0A               asl
00012Cr 2  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
00012Er 2  0A               asl
00012Fr 2  0A               asl
000130r 2  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000132r 2  84 DE            sty RSEED+3                     ; finish rotating byte 2 into 3
000134r 2  85 DB            sta RSEED+0
000136r 2  60               rts
000137r 2               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------
000137r 2               ;                        NUMBER CONVERSIONS
000137r 2               ;
000137r 2               DEC2ASCII:       ;  X is # - return as two digits in Z_MATH3, Z_MATH3+1
000137r 2  A9 30            lda #'0'
000139r 2  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
00013Br 2  8A               txa
00013Cr 2  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
00013Er 2               D2ASCLOOP:
00013Er 2  38               sec
00013Fr 2  E9 0A            sbc #$0A
000141r 2  90 04            bcc D2ASCNEXT
000143r 2  E6 B7            inc Z_MATH3+1
000145r 2  80 F7            bra D2ASCLOOP
000147r 2               D2ASCNEXT:
000147r 2  18               clc
000148r 2  69 3A            adc #$3A
00014Ar 2  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
00014Cr 2  60               rts
00014Dr 2               ;
00014Dr 2               ;      more math (from bios.s)
00014Dr 2               ;
00014Dr 2               ; MATH
00014Dr 2               MOD_10:

00014Fr 2  C9 00                    cmp             #0
000151r 2  30 14                    bmi             @negative
000153r 2  C9 64                    cmp             #100
000155r 2  30 05                    bmi             @pos_loop
000157r 2  A2 0A                    ldx             #10
000159r 2  38                       sec
00015Ar 2  E9 64                    sbc             #100
00015Cr 2               
00015Cr 2               @pos_loop:
00015Cr 2  C9 0A                    cmp             #10
00015Er 2  30 06                    bmi             @pos_end
000160r 2  E8                       inx
000161r 2  38                       sec
000162r 2  E9 0A                    sbc             #10
000164r 2  10 F6                    bpl             @pos_loop
000166r 2               
000166r 2               @pos_end:
000166r 2  60                       rts
000167r 2               
000167r 2               @negative:
000167r 2  C9 A6                    cmp             #$A6            ; -90
000169r 2  10 02                    bpl             @neg_start
00016Br 2  69 A6                    adc             #$A6
00016Dr 2               
00016Dr 2               @neg_start:
00016Dr 2  18                       clc
00016Er 2               
00016Er 2               @neg_loop:
00016Er 2  69 0A                    adc             #10
000170r 2  30 FC                    bmi             @neg_loop
000172r 2  60                       rts
000173r 2               
000173r 2               REM_10:
000173r 2  A2 00                    ldx             #0
000175r 2  C9 00                    cmp             #0
000177r 2  30 14                    bmi             @negative

0001B1r 2               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0001B1r 2               ;                                   MEMCPY
0001B1r 2               ;    load mainoff ZP_TEMP_VEC -> ZP_TEMP_VEC2, ZP_TEMP_VEC3 is end of copy
0001B1r 2               ;     with current ROM offsets (main = start, ends = finish)
0001B1r 2               ;     and ramstart with destination corresponding to main.
0001B1r 2               ;     Set 'supprint' <> 0 (TEMP5 $FC) to suppress printing of progress bar from Forth
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;    reworked to used ZP_TEMP_VEC, ZP_TEMP_VEC2, ZP_TEMP_VEC3
0001B1r 2               ;                     mainoff      endsoff       ramstart
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ; Dan's BIOS Memory Copy
0001B1r 2               ; was ZP_TEMP_VEC: From, ZP_TEMP_VEC2: To, .A.Y: LSB/MSB size
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               MEMCPY_AY:                     ; to obviate Dan's version and have a length initialized version
0001B1r 2  48               pha
0001B2r 2  A5 rr            lda MC_ENDA                           ; dest in AY version
0001B4r 2  85 rr            sta MC_DEST                           ; copy to dest
0001B6r 2  A5 rr            lda MC_ENDA+1
0001B8r 2  85 rr            sta MC_DEST+1
0001BAr 2  68               pla
0001BBr 2  18               clc
0001BCr 2  65 rr            adc MC_SRCA                           ; add source addr
0001BEr 2  85 rr            sta MC_ENDA
0001C0r 2  98               tya
0001C1r 2  65 rr            adc MC_SRCA+1
0001C3r 2  85 rr            sta MC_ENDA+1
0001C5r 2  B0 2C            bcs CPY_RTS                           ; someone fucked up, bail out
0001C7r 2               MEMCPY:
0001C7r 2  DA               phx                                   ; preserve X
0001C8r 2  A0 00            ldy  #0
0001CAr 2               COPYLOOP:
0001CAr 2  B1 rr            lda (MC_SRCA),y                       ; source address
0001CCr 2  91 rr            sta (MC_DEST),y                       ; dest address
0001CEr 2  A5 rr            lda MC_SRCA
0001D0r 2  C5 rr            cmp MC_ENDA                            ; compare to end address LSB
0001D2r 2  D0 06            bne SKIP1
0001D4r 2  A5 rr            lda MC_SRCA+1                          ; and the MSB
0001D6r 2  C5 rr            cmp MC_ENDA+1
0001D8r 2  F0 17            beq CPY_XRTS
0001DAr 2               SKIP1:
0001DAr 2  A5 FC            lda supprint                           ; suppress progress bar if supprint != 0
0001DCr 2  D0 05            bne SKIP2
0001DEr 2  A9 2E            lda #'.'
0001E0r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0001E3r 2               SKIP2:
0001E3r 2  E6 rr            inc MC_SRCA                           ; increment source addr
0001E5r 2  D0 02            bne SKIP3
0001E7r 2  E6 rr            inc MC_SRCA+1
0001E9r 2               SKIP3:
0001E9r 2  E6 rr            inc MC_DEST                            ; increment dest
0001EBr 2  D0 02            bne SKIP4
0001EDr 2  E6 rr            inc MC_DEST+1
0001EFr 2               SKIP4:
0001EFr 2  80 D9            bra COPYLOOP
0001F1r 2               CPY_XRTS:
0001F1r 2  18               clc                                    ; all went well?
0001F2r 2  FA               plx                                    ; bring back X
0001F3r 2               CPY_RTS:
0001F3r 2  60               rts

0003A6r 2               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------
0003A6r 2               ;
0003A6r 2               ;  ANSI CLS, cursor reposition, and color change
0003A6r 2               ;
0003A6r 2               ;    ANSIONEDIG expects ASCII coded digits in Z_ANSI1, ones:tens little-endian
0003A6r 2               ;                        e.g. $3031 = '10'
0003A6r 2               ;
0003A6r 2               ANSIHDR:
0003A6r 2  A9 1B            lda #27
0003A8r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0003ABr 2  A9 5B            lda #91
0003ADr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0003B0r 2  60               rts
0003B1r 2               
0003B1r 2               ANSIONEDIG:   ;two ASCII digits in Z_ANSI1
0003B1r 2  A5 BB            lda Z_ANSI1 + 1
0003B3r 2  C9 30            cmp #$30
0003B5r 2  F0 03            beq ansi_scr_00
0003B7r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0003BAr 2               ansi_scr_00:
0003BAr 2  A5 BA            lda Z_ANSI1
0003BCr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0003BFr 2  60               rts
0003C0r 2               ;
0003C0r 2               CLEAR_SCR:
0003C0r 2               ANSICLS:
0003C0r 2  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIHDR
0003C3r 2  A9 32            lda #'2'
0003C5r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0003C8r 2  A9 4A            lda #'J'
0003CAr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0003CDr 2  60               rts
0003CEr 2               
0003CEr 2               ANSISCR:      ; assumes Z_ANSI1 AND Z_ANSI2 have digits
0003CEr 2                             ; one or two digit ASCII #'s already
0003CEr 2  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIHDR
0003D1r 2  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIONEDIG
0003D4r 2  A9 3B            lda #';'
0003D6r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0003D9r 2  A5 BC            lda Z_ANSI2
0003DBr 2  85 BA            sta Z_ANSI1
0003DDr 2  A5 BD            lda Z_ANSI2+1
0003DFr 2  85 BB            sta Z_ANSI1+1
0003E1r 2  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIONEDIG
0003E4r 2  A9 66            lda #'f'
0003E6r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0003E9r 2  60               rts
0003EAr 2               
0003EAr 2               ANSICOL:                 ; assumes Z_ANSI1 has digits
0003EAr 2  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIHDR
0003EDr 2  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIONEDIG
0003F0r 2  A9 6D            lda #'m'
0003F2r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR

00010Cr 2               ;            sta                 V_REGISTER
00010Cr 2               ;            brk                                                     ; force an interrupt
00010Cr 2               ;            rts
00010Cr 2               ;
00010Cr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
00010Cr 2               ;
00010Cr 2               ;           OS PAGE FILLERS
00010Cr 2               ;
00010Cr 2               .macro OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
00010Cr 2                           lda     #0                                              ; zero out W register
00010Cr 2                           sta     W_REGISTER
00010Cr 2                           .res                $1FF2, $EA
00010Cr 2                           jmp                 RESET_VECTOR_START
00010Cr 2               .endmacro
